Conclusion: 当前的图基础模型（GFM）领域缺乏统一的评估标准。研究发现仅有极少数基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在节点属性预测任务中能超越精心调参后的传统图神经网络（GNN），且往往伴随着更高的推理成本。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [141] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将 LLM 的复杂推理视为进入高维联想记忆吸引子盆地的“物理弛豫”过程，通过基于谱熵的能量加权采样机制，有效抑制了幻觉并提升了数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 不应仅被视为自回归生成器，而应被视为高维稠密联想记忆（Dense Associative Memories）。解决推理中“幻觉”与“正确路径”在能量景观中分布差异的问题，即正确推理对应深而宽的吸引子盆地（平坦极小值），而幻觉对应尖锐且不稳定的局部极小值。

Method: 提出了一种基于吉布斯测度（Gibbs measure）的路径检索机制，利用轨迹的谱熵（spectral entropy）作为能量函数，通过对多条推理路径进行采样并根据逆能量权重（$P \propto e^{-βE}$）进行加权，从而逼近联想记忆的平衡分布。

Result: 该方法在 Microsoft Phi-3.5 模型上进行测试，GSM8K 基准测试性能由 84.7% 提升至 90.1%，提升了 5.38%。

Conclusion: 将推理过程建模为动态系统中的“能量势能面寻找”过程（即收敛至吸引子盆地），比传统的贪婪式预测更精准且具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [142] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QC-SMOTE是一种新型质量可控的过采样框架，通过评估样本可靠性并自适应地调整合成策略，在处理不平衡数据时有效避免了过拟合噪声，显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过采样方法（如SMOTE）在处理具有噪声或类重叠的数据不平衡问题时，往往会生成低质量的合成样本，进而影响分类器的性能。

Method: 提出QC-SMOTE框架，包含：1.基于局部密度、安全水平和隔离度的样本可靠性评估；2.基于IPQ（纯度引导）的Best-of-K合成生成策略；3.基于局部纯度阈值的自适应采样行为调整；4.低质量样本自动替换机制（当局部纯度过低时退化为样本复制）。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上的实验表明，QC-SMOTE在AUC-ROC和Macro F1指标上优于主流对比方法，尤其在中度和重度不平衡场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: QC-SMOTE通过引入质量控制机制和自适应采样策略，有效克服了传统过采样方法（如SMOTE）在噪声和类重叠区域产生低质量样本的问题，显著提升了不平衡数据分类的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [143] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe 是一种基于条件生成流网络 (C-GFlowNets) 的数据准备流水线自动合成框架，通过结合 LLM 语义先验与失败感知探索机制，显著提升了流水线构建的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动流水线生成方法（如 Multi-DQN）存在信用分配困难、数据集上下文注入薄弱以及在稀疏搜索空间中探索效率低的问题，导致流水线构建的质量和效率受限。

Method: 1. 将流水线合成建模为基于有向无环图的条件概率流生成任务；2. 使用条件生成流网络 (C-GFlowNets) 和轨迹平衡目标 (Trajectory Balance) 优化长期信用分配；3. 引入基于 FiLM 的深度语义调制，将 LLM 提取的逻辑先验注入策略；4. 设计具备失败感知机制的流目标，以规避无效状态。

Result: 在 74 个真实数据集基准测试中，FlowPipe 平均准确率提升了 11.96%，且训练收敛速度较现有 SOTA 方法快 12.5 倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe 通过将自动数据准备流水线建模为条件生成流网络，有效解决了多步决策中的信用分配、语义上下文整合及无效状态探索等关键挑战，在性能和收敛效率上均优于现有的 SOTA 方法。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [144] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Grad Detect，一种通过分析 LLM 推理过程中的层级梯度模式来检测幻觉的新方法，显著优于传统方法，并展示了通过重点监控模型后端层级即可实现高效的可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）能力强大，但仍存在严重的幻觉问题，这限制了其在关键领域的应用。亟需一种不依赖仅输出信号、能够提供更深层可靠性分析的幻觉检测机制。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Grad Detect”的梯度分析方法。该方法通过在推理阶段进行单次前向-反向传播，分析模型层级的梯度模式，并提取出内部梯度结构信息来预测幻觉。

Result: 在多个问答基准测试中，Grad Detect 在幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上均优于现有的基于置信度和采样的基准方法。此外，研究发现模型最后五层包含了超过 97% 的判别性梯度信号，实现了高预测性能与高效计算的平衡。

Conclusion: Grad Detect 证明了模型内部梯度特征在检测幻觉方面优于传统的置信度或采样方法。最终五层集中了绝大部分判别性信号，使其不仅高效且具有良好的可解释性，为 LLM 的可靠性提供了有力的评估工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [145] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入哈特利神经算子（HNO）并将其与傅里叶神经算子（FNO）对比，提出根据 PDE 的相位对称性选择谱基的原则，打破了 FNO 作为通用解决方案的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 在处理实值 PDE 时，因复数共轭对称性存在表征冗余。作者旨在探索使用完全实数的谱变换（Hartley 变换）是否能在特定 PDE 类型上实现更高效的模拟，并探讨谱基选择对算子性能的影响。

Method: 提出哈特利神经算子（HNO），利用离散哈特利变换（DHT）替代 FNO 中的离散傅里叶变换（DFT）。HNO 在全实数域进行卷积运算，避免了复数乘法的冗余，并在参数量相同的情况下与 FNO 等效。通过对比实验及格林函数理论分析，验证了算子性能与 PDE 相位特性的关系。

Result: HNO 在椭圆型方程（如泊松、双调和方程）中表现优异；FNO 在包含相位信息的演化方程（如波动、Burgers、Navier-Stokes 方程）中表现更好。实验验证了算子性能与算子相位含量之间的单调演化关系。

Conclusion: 不存在通用的最优神经算子。研究提出了一个预测性法则：应根据算子的对称性（如是否包含相位信息）来选择谱基。对于自伴随的椭圆型方程，实值的 Hartley 变换（HNO）更优；对于包含相位传递的演化型方程，复值的傅里叶变换（FNO）更优。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Is competitive online paging an artifact?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23955)
*Enoch Peserico,Michele Scquizzato*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 现有的竞争分析模型（如Sleator-Tarjan）因忽略了新数据产生于处理器的物理本质而存在根本缺陷；修正该模型后，在线分页算法在理论上无法实现竞争性，这挑战了现有缓存算法框架（如缓存无关算法）的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出目前广泛使用的Sleator-Tarjan分页模型与实际计算系统存在矛盾：真实系统中新数据产生于处理器而非外部存储，因此不应产生“冷启动缺失（cold miss）”。作者旨在揭示现有竞争分析理论模型的失准及其对相关缓存算法框架的深远影响。

Method: 通过修正Sleator-Tarjan模型（对首次访问新生成数据不收费），重新审视在线分页算法的竞争比。分析了包括随机化算法、资源增强等在内的多种变体，并考察了其对理想缓存模型（和缓存无关/缓存自适应算法）的影响。

Result: 修正模型后，证明了即便使用随机化算法、进行资源增强，也不存在任何具有竞争力的在线分页算法。这表明现有的竞争分析理论在面对现实计算序列时是失效的。

Conclusion: 现行的竞争分析模型（如Sleator-Tarjan模型）及其衍生模型（如理想缓存模型）存在根本性的缺陷。它们忽略了“新生成数据初始存储在处理器中”这一事实，导致预测结果在理论上难以成立。作者认为，现有的竞争分析对LRU等缓存算法的良好性能预测可能只是一种“侥幸的失真”，而非理论上的必然。

Abstract: In any real system a newly computed datum begins its existence in the processor rather than in external memory, and thus does not inevitably incur a cold miss. This was captured by early I/O models, but not by the Sleator-Tarjan one that has come to underpin competitive analysis of paging. If one corrects the Sleator-Tarjan model by charging no cost for the first access to newly computed data, optimal offline algorithms such as LFD remain optimal, but no online paging algorithm can be competitive, even if randomized, even with arbitrary resource augmentation, even against request sequences that are not tailored against it but are instead representative of widely used computational techniques. The proofs are simple, and appear robust against any reasonable assumption/model adjustment, including virtually all tools developed to make competitive analysis less pessimistic.
  In other words, while competitive analysis does predict the good performance exhibited in practice by online paging algorithms such as LRU, these predictions seem just a fortuitous artifact of an incorrect assumption that has crept into the underlying model several decades ago. And there are implications beyond paging, too: for example, the same issue undermines the Ideal Cache model on which the popular Cache-Oblivious and Cache-Adaptive algorithmic frameworks are based.

</details>


### [147] [A Near-Optimal Parallel Algorithm for Finding Matroid Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24845)
*Sanjeev Khanna,Aaron Putterman,Junkai Song*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究者提出了一种新的并行算法，在 $O(n^{1/3}\log^{1/3}n)$ 轮次内解决了拟阵基计算问题，几乎达到了理论最优下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 Karp、Upfal 和 Wigderson 在其 1985 年开创性工作中提出的关于计算拟阵基的并行复杂性这一经典问题。

Method: 设计了一种在 $O(n^{1/3}\log^{1/3}n)$ 轮次内运行的并行算法。

Result: 算法运行时间为 $O(n^{1/3}\log^{1/3}n)$，与 KUW 提出的下界相比，仅存在 $O(\log^{2/3}n)$ 的因子差距。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了拟阵基计算的并行复杂性经典问题，并达到了KUW下界（仅相差对数因子）。

Abstract: We settle the classic question of the parallel complexity of computing a matroid basis, as first posed in the seminal work of Karp, Upfal, and Wigderson (FOCS 1985, JCSS 1988). Our algorithm runs in $O(n^{1/3}\log^{1/3}n)$ rounds, matching the lower bound of KUW up to a $\log^{2/3}(n)$ factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Verifiable Foundation Models for Robot Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23754)
*Davide Corsi,Kyungmin Kim,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FEARL 通过将机器人策略分解为“大型感知控制器”与“小型可验证安全模块”，成功解决了大模型难以进行形式化安全验证的问题，实现了高性能与强安全性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（Foundation Models）虽提供强大的感知能力，但由于模型极其复杂且黑盒，难以直接进行形式化验证，导致在对安全性要求极高的机器人控制任务中难以直接部署。

Method: 提出了一种名为 FEARL 的模块化架构，将策略解构为负责高维感知与推理的大型控制器（Controller）和负责执行低维安全约束的小型安全模块（Safety module）；利用形式化验证方法仅对安全模块进行约束，确保任务执行满足预定的安全指标。

Result: 在三个仿真机器人域及真实的物理机器人实验中验证了该框架，实验表明 FEARL 在保持任务执行能力的前提下，成功实现了安全特性的形式化可验证性，支持从仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的有效迁移。

Conclusion: FEARL 通过模块化架构有效地在保持基础模型强大感知与推理能力的同时，实现了机器人控制系统的形式化安全验证，为复杂机器人系统的部署提供了一种可行的安全保证路径。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models for robot control raises a central challenge: the expressive power that enables rich, multimodal perception also makes these models opaque and difficult to analyze formally, rendering them intractable for existing verification tools. In this paper, we present FEARL (Foundation-Enabled Assured Robot Learning), a framework that addresses this tension through a modular architectural decomposition. FEARL separates the policy into a large Controller (C) responsible for high-dimensional perception and task reasoning, and a small Safety module (S) that receives low-dimensional observations from dedicated safety sensors together with a bounded context embedding from C and produces the final action. Since many robot safety requirements, such as collision avoidance and workspace boundary constraints, can be expressed over these safety sensor observations, formal verification can be applied to S rather than to the full foundation-model backbone. This makes formal analysis tractable with existing tools while preserving the Controller's expressive power for task reasoning. To show that the decomposed policy remains capable of solving diverse tasks, we evaluate FEARL on three simulated robotic domains using multiple Controller backbones and training procedures, including pretrained off-the-shelf vision-language-action models. We further transfer the learned policy from one of our simulated tasks to a physical robot, suggesting that the low-dimensional safety interface supports practical sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [149] [Engineering Reliable Autonomous Systems: Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23760)
*Marie Farrell,Matt Luckcuck,Angelo Ferrando,Rafael C. Cardoso,Natasha Alechina,Marco Autili,Diana Benjumea Hernandez,Luciana Brasil Rebelo dos Santos,Daniela Briola,Ana Cavalcanti,Christian Colombo,Louise A. Dennis,Clare Dixon,Michael Fisher,Mario Gleirscher,Taylor Johnson,Charles Lesire,Livia Lestingi,Sven Linker,Brian Logan,Colin Paterson,Fabio Papacchini,Patrizio Pelliccione,Pedro Ribeiro,Maike Schwammberger,Silvia Lizeth Tapia Tarifa,Hazel Taylor,Jim Woodcock,Mengwei Xu,Yi Yang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文总结了ERAS研讨会成果，针对自主系统的验证、工程化及架构设计，通过产学结合梳理了当前关键挑战，并制定了从学术成果转化到前沿技术攻关的系统性路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主系统日益普及，亟需提出更易于使用且可靠的工程实践手段，以应对复杂自主系统在验证、实际部署及软件架构设计方面的挑战。

Method: 通过组织跨学科研讨会（ERAS），汇集形式化方法（FMAS）与智能体/机器人（AREA）领域的专家、工业界从业者，共同讨论并梳理工程化自主系统中的核心挑战与解决方案。

Result: 整理并形成了一份关于自主系统工程的挑战目录及解决方案路线图，明确了哪些现有学术技术可应用于工业实践，以及哪些领域尚需进一步探索研究。

Conclusion: 该报告确立了一份涵盖验证与验证技术、实际部署及软件架构的挑战目录，并为弥合学术界与工业界之间的鸿沟提供了行动规划，旨在指导未来的研究方向与产学协作。

Abstract: Engineering reliable autonomous systems is an important and growing topic in computer science. As autonomous systems become more prevalent, easy-to-use techniques for building them reliably are increasingly important.
  This workshop report captures and expands on the discussions at the Lorentz Center Workshop "Engineering Reliable Autonomous Systems" (ERAS), held from 10 to 14 June 2024. The workshop was co-organised by the organisers of the Workshop on Formal Methods for Autonomous Systems (FMAS) and the Workshop on Agents and Robots for reliable Engineered Autonomy (AREA). It brought together members of the FMAS and AREA communities, industry practitioners, and representatives from sectors where autonomous systems pose distinctive engineering challenges.
  The workshop focused on three main research topics: techniques for verification and validation of autonomous systems; engineering real-world autonomous systems; and software architectures for safe autonomous systems. Its main outcome is a catalogue of challenges in these areas and, most importantly, a pathway to solutions. Some challenges can already be tackled by techniques that are well known in academia but have not yet become regularly used in practice. Other challenges remain unresolved and require further research. This roadmap is intended to support future research and industrial collaboration.

</details>


### [150] [Enforcing Human-like Kinematics in Dexterous Piano Playing via Adversarial Posture Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23848)
*Bin Qiu,Yanming Shao,Guanyu Cai,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机械手钢琴演奏中姿态不自然的问题，提出APR方法，通过对抗学习引入少量人类先验，在保持高准确率的同时显著提升了动作的人类相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用强化学习训练高自由度机械手演奏钢琴时，仅依赖任务奖励或逆运动学（IK）控制容易导致姿态生硬、关节过度伸展等不自然现象，缺乏人体相似度。

Method: 提出“对抗性姿态正则化（APR）”方法。该方法不依赖昂贵的任务对齐专家数据，而是引入少量非结构化的人类钢琴演奏视频数据。通过对抗性目标函数，强制模型输出的指尖/手势姿态分布与人类先验分布对齐。此外，使用Meta Quest 3采集并重定向运动数据以辅助训练。

Result: 在所有三个评价指标（cPSI, BSE, FAC）上均优于基准方法，且视觉表现更加自然流畅。

Conclusion: APR框架通过强化学习与对抗性姿态正则化，在无需大量精细标注数据的情况下，显著提升了高自由度机械手演奏钢琴动作的自然度与人体相似性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train bimanual dexterous hands to play piano in physics simulation with high note accuracy, but for high-DoF dexterous hands, relying solely on task rewards or IK inversion often leads to unnatural postures and joint overextension. We propose \textit{Adversarial Posture Regularization (APR)}. It avoids expensive, song-aligned expert demonstration data and instead uses a small amount of casual human playing data. By matching the distribution of the posture of the policy with the human prior through an adversarial objective, APR encourages more human-like hand shapes. Meanwhile, we collect and release unstructured hand motion data of piano playing using a consumer-grade Meta Quest 3, and retarget the key motion information to the Shadow Hand. Finally, we achieve significantly better performance than prior methods on all three human-likeness metrics (cPSI, BSE, and FAC) as well as in visual quality. Project repository: https://github.com/APRProject/APRPianist.

</details>


### [151] [PDS Joint: A Parametric Double-Spiral Joint Tailored for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24377)
*Haoyang Li,Yibo Wen,Yixiang Fan,Yiheng Xu,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化双螺旋（PDS）柔性关节，通过几何设计实现多维度刚度调控，并结合嵌入式感应器与机器学习校准技术，解决了灵巧手在大行程运动中的刚度控制与状态感知难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性关节在实现灵巧手大行程仿生运动的同时，难以兼顾特定方向的刚度调控和可靠的本体感知，限制了机器人在复杂交互环境下的应用。

Method: 1. 设计基于阿基米德和对数螺旋模板的PDS关节，利用不对称比例调节多自由度下的刚度分布；2. 集成嵌入式感应式传感器以提供本体感知；3. 采用基于ArUco标记跟踪的机器学习管线（MLP）校准关节状态，将感知误差较传统曲线拟合降低41.6%。

Result: 1. 建立了PDS关节刚度景观与结构参数的关系；2. 开发了精度更高的关节状态估计模型（MLP）；3. 在开源灵巧手上验证了抓取9种日常物品及安全的人机交互能力。

Conclusion: PDS关节为灵巧手提供了一种实现大行程、可编程方向性刚度和可靠本体感知的有效解决方案，证明了通过结构几何设计与学习型感知融合可提升机器人手的交互能力和安全性。

Abstract: Compliant joints can embed safety and adaptability into dexterous hands, but achieving large-stroke anthropomorphic motion while maintaining joint-specific, directiondependent stiffness and reliable proprioception remains challenging. This paper presents the PDS joint, a parametric doublespiral (PDS) compliant joint that enables systematic shaping of directional stiffness across multiple deformation modes, including flexion/extension, abduction/adduction, and pronation/supination. We instantiate the joint using Archimedean and logarithmic spiral templates for different hand joints and introduce an asymmetry ratio to tailor stiffness distributions for both grasp stability and hyperextension resistance. To make the joint practically usable under large deformation, we co-design embedded inductive proprioception and propose a learningbased calibration pipeline that maps raw inductive signals to joint states using ArUco-marker tracking. Experiments characterize the stiffness landscapes across geometric parameters and demonstrate a non-monotonic dependence of lateral support on asymmetry, indicating the importance of principled parameter tuning. For joint-state estimation in the most challenging abduction/adduction motion, a learned multilayer-perceptron (MLP) mapping reduces the error compared with conventional curve fitting by 41.6%. Finally, we integrate the proposed joints into an open-source dexterous hand as a demonstration platform, on which the hand grasps a set of nine everyday objects and performs safe, contact-rich human-involved interactions.

</details>


### [152] [Topological Online Learning for Displacement-based Formation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23901)
*Saksham Sharma,Shubhankar Gupta,Sumant A Gunagi,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑在线学习（TOLD）的队形控制框架，通过实时动态调整机器人间的交互权重来最小化队形畸变，在理论验证、仿真及四旋翼无人机实物实验中均表现出优于传统固定拓扑的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统鲁棒控制器仅调节节点输入、无法调整通信/交互拓扑的问题，旨在通过实时调节拓扑结构来增强队形控制的鲁棒性。

Method: 引入拓扑在线学习（TOLD）框架，提出在线梯度流（OGF，无约束权重）和在线指数梯度流（OExpGF，非负凸权重）两种策略，通过在线更新边缘交互权重来减小队形畸变。

Result: 仿真显示累计根均方畸变误差（RMDE）降低了1.2%-33.14%；硬件实验证明，相比固定权重方案，OGF和OExpGF分别减少了超过62%和31.4%的队形畸变。

Conclusion: TOLD框架通过动态调整交互拓扑权重，能有效提升多机器人系统在扰动下的队形保持能力。该方法不仅在理论上保证了收敛性和稳定性，还在仿真及硬件实验中显著优于传统的固定权重控制策略。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of robust formation control by introducing Topological Online Learning for Displacement-based (TOLD) formation control, a real-time edge-level adaptation framework. Unlike conventional node-level robust controllers that regulate individual robot inputs without modifying the interaction topology, TOLD updates the interaction topology weights online to directly minimize formation distortion. Two strategies are proposed under the TOLD formation control framework: Online Gradient Flow (OGF) with unconstrained weights and Online Exponential Gradient Flow (OExpGF) with non-negative convex weights. Theoretical analysis establishes that, for single-integrator agents over directed graphs, OExpGF guarantees asymptotic consensus, while OGF ensures bounded formation distortion. Simulations with twelve robots under intermittent disturbances show 1.2%-33.14% median cumulative Root Mean Distortion Error reduction when augmenting TOLD with node-level controllers. Hardware experiments with Crazyflie 2.0 quadrotors demonstrate over 62% (OGF) and 31.4% (OExpGF) reduction in median formation distortion compared to fixed-weight consensus.

</details>


### [153] [Sim-to-Real Betting on the E-Process: Bringing "simulators" to anytime-valid confidence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24038)
*Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文整合了博弈论与随时有效推断方法，为机器人仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的性能测试提供了一种高效且具备统计可靠性的均值估计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真到现实的性能估计中缺乏兼顾效率与可靠性的方法，特别是在机器人性能测试场景下，需要一种既能保证预测安全性，又能随时进行有效推断的机制。

Method: 整合了Chen et al.提出的“博弈论框架（betting）”与Ramdas et al.提出的“安全随时有效推断”，并结合缩放模拟器（scaled simulators）技术，构建了一个用于机器人性能评估的综合估计框架。

Result: 提出了一个自包含的算法框架，通过合成示例验证了其有效性，并提供了开源实现路径，能够为机器人系统的性能评估产生可靠的置信区间或证书。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一种将仿真到现实（sim-to-real）性能估计与博弈论框架及安全随时有效推断（safe anytime-valid inference）相结合的方法，证明了该方法在机器人性能评估中能够生成高效且可靠的均值估计证书。

Abstract: This note describes an integration of the sim-to-real performance estimate with betting (from Chen et al.) and the safe anytime-valid inference (from Ramdas et al.). Using the scaled simulators. The method produces efficient, reliable certificates for the mean estimate, an approach that is especially valuable in robot performance testing. This note gives a primary, self-contained account of the construction; preliminaries of the respective methods are kept at a minimum, and one shall refer to the original works for full detail. Some synthetic examples demonstrating the proposed algorithm can be found at https://github.com/ISUSAIL/Bet4Sim2Real-EProcess.

</details>


### [154] [TurboMPC: Fast, Scalable, and Differentiable Model Predictive Control on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24039)
*Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Jianghan Zhang,Zachary Pestrikov,Brian Plancher,Thomas Lew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TurboMPC是一个完全在GPU上运行的、高度可微且支持复杂约束的MPC求解器，通过创新的算法结合与硬件加速，显著提升了大规模机器人控制与学习任务的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人技术向大规模并行仿真和学习方向发展，传统的MPC求解器在GPU协同、可微性和处理大规模复杂约束能力上存在不足，难以满足高性能控制需求。

Method: 结合了顺序二次规划（SQP）、乘法交替方向法（ADMM）作为内层求解器、隐式微分技术，并采用JAX-CUDA进行深度协同设计以最大化并行效率。

Result: 在仿真中实现了较现有最先进CPU求解器快15倍、GPU求解器快58倍的性能；具备处理超过8000个节点长规划周期的能力；成功应用于实车自动驾驶比赛，通过大规模参数优化实现了超越人工调参的性能。

Conclusion: TurboMPC实现了在GPU上高效、可微且支持复杂约束的MPC求解，显著提升了机器人任务的计算速度和扩展性，验证了其在复杂控制和学习任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Robotics increasingly relies on GPUs for parallel simulation, large-scale learning, and neural-network inference. For model predictive control (MPC) to scale with this paradigm, solvers must run efficiently on this hardware while remaining fast, differentiable, and compatible with expressive MPC formulations used in robotics. We present TurboMPC, a differentiable MPC solver that runs entirely on the GPU and supports state and control inequality constraints, implicit integrators, cross-time-coupled costs, and slack variables. TurboMPC combines sequential quadratic programming (SQP), an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver, implicit differentiation, and a co-designed JAX-CUDA implementation for efficiency and ease of use. In simulation, we validate TurboMPC on constrained planning, humanoid imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with neural-network cost function tasks, achieving up to $15\times$ and $58\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art CPU and GPU differentiable solvers, respectively. We deploy TurboMPC on a full-scale car for minimum-time racing and find that batched, GPU-accelerated tuning of MPC parameters via Bayesian optimization yields significantly faster driving than a hand-tuned baseline. TurboMPC also scales to planning horizons of over $8000$ knot points while maintaining control of the vehicle. We open-source TurboMPC at: https://github.com/ToyotaResearchInstitute/turbompc

</details>


### [155] [SPACE: Enabling Learning from Cross-Robot Data Toward Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24049)
*Haeone Lee,Byeongguk Jeon,Suchae Jeong,Jian Kim,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决不同机器人形态导致的训练数据互不兼容问题，本文提出了SPACE框架，利用通用的笛卡尔状态增量作为中间表达，解耦了策略执行与具体底层的机器人控制，从而提升了多机器人协作与适应环境变化的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学习中，不同实体的动作对相同运动任务的需求不同，传统的行为克隆（直接预测原始控制命令）难以在不同机器人及复杂变化环境下泛化。

Method: 提出了SPACE框架，包含两部分：1. 使用笛卡尔状态增量（Cartesian state delta）作为通用的动作表示，训练预测几何末端执行器位移的策略；2. 设计动作适配器（Action Adapter），将预测的通用位移转换为特定机器人的控制命令。

Result: 在跨形态、跨硬件单元的实验中，SPACE显著优于直接预测控制命令的策略，并在控制频率、负载变化及控制器增益调整等动态偏移下展现出极强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SPACE框架通过解耦策略（几何位移预测）与执行（机器人特定命令转换），有效地解决了跨形态、跨硬件及动态变化下的机器人策略学习难题，证明了该方法在泛化能力和鲁棒性上的优越性。

Abstract: In robot learning, scaling training datasets across diverse embodiments and environments has become a dominant paradigm for learning generalizable robot policies. These policies are commonly trained via behavior cloning to imitate actions from pre-collected demonstrations. However, since robot actions are tied to the dynamics of the data collection robot, different robots may require different actions to achieve the same motion. This discrepancy hinders both policy training and deployment across diverse robots. To address this, we propose using Cartesian state delta as a universal action representation across robots, and introduce State Prediction and Adaptive Command Execution (SPACE) framework. SPACE handles robot dynamics variation at three levels: across different embodiments, across hardware units of the same embodiment, and within a single robot during operation. It consists of two components: (i) a Cartesian state delta policy that predicts geometric end-effector displacement, and (ii) Action Adapter, which converts the predicted Cartesian state delta into robot-specific control commands. Experiments show that SPACE substantially outperforms policies that directly predict control commands when learning from data collected across different embodiments and across hardware units of the same embodiment. SPACE also remains robust under dynamics shifts at deployment, including changes in control frequency, object weight, and controller gains. The project page is available at http://haeone.site/space-website/.

</details>


### [156] [MinInter: Minimizing Trajectory Interpolation During Data Augmentation for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24078)
*Qingyang Wang,Xingang Liu,Changwei Yao,Zikai Ouyang,Junwei Liu,Haibo Lu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MinInter方法，通过在机器人数据增强过程中优先选择插值需求最少的演示路径，有效解决了合成数据质量低的问题，并提升了策略学习的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹级数据增强方法通常会在不相交的演示片段间插入插值或非专家过渡段，导致生成的合成数据质量下降，限制了机器人模仿学习的效果。

Method: 提出了MinInter（最小化插值）方法，在进行轨迹级数据增强时，为每个采样的初始配置选择所需插值最少的一段源演示，从而形成完整轨迹，减少非专家过渡段对数据质量的负面影响。

Result: 在MimicGen基准测试的12个任务26个变体上，MinInter显著提升了数据生成成功率和策略成功率，特别是在接触密集、长视角和高方差的任务中表现突出，效果优于SkillGen框架。

Conclusion: 通过最小化插值来生成高质量的合成演示数据，是在模仿学习中提升策略性能的有效且简洁的手段，特别是在复杂任务场景下具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex manipulation skills from demonstrations, but its effectiveness is limited by the cost of collecting high-quality data. Trajectory-level data augmentation methods alleviate this challenge by recombining expert demonstrations under varied initial states. However, such methods typically insert interpolations or other non-expert transition segments between disjoint parts, and such non-expert segments could reduce the quality of the generated data. This paper introduces Minimizing Interpolation (MinInter), an effective trajectory selection method that, for each sampled initial configuration, chooses the source demonstration requiring the least interpolation to form a complete trajectory. By explicitly minimizing interpolations during data generation, MinInter produces higher-quality synthetic demonstrations while remaining compatible with existing data generation frameworks. Experiments on 12 manipulation tasks with 26 variants from the MimicGen benchmark show that MinInter consistently improves both data generation success rates and policy success rates, with the largest gains on contact-rich, long-horizon and high-variance settings. Compared to the recent SkillGen framework, MinInter achieves higher policy success rates despite its conceptual simplicity, underscoring the value of interpolation minimization for data augmentation.

</details>


### [157] [DynaWM: Dynamics-Aware Distillation with World Model and Momentum Targets for Smooth Locomotion over Continuous Stairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24089)
*Haidong Hou,Zhangguo Yu,Hengbo Qi,Jianlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决双足轮式机器人在复杂楼梯场景下的感知与适应难题，本文提出了 DynaWM 框架，通过引入动态感知世界模型和动量蒸馏机制，增强了机器人的地形编码能力与运动平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双足轮式机器人控制框架在应对长距离楼梯时，存在地形几何编码信息缺失和动态感知表征能力减弱的问题，限制了机器人的通行能力。

Method: 1. 提出动态感知（dynamics-aware）表征学习框架 DynaWM；2. 引入世界模型作为正则化器，强制前向动态感知，以优化地形几何编码；3. 采用动量目标编码器来稳定知识蒸馏过程，解决非平稳教师模型更新导致的维度坍塌问题。

Result: 通过 PCA 可视化和定量指标证明了该编码器能分层捕获地形几何信息；实验表明，该方法在仿真和真实硬件上均实现了更优的地形适应性和运动平滑性，成功克服了连续楼梯挑战。

Conclusion: DynaWM 框架通过引入动态感知世界模型和动量目标编码器，有效提升了双足轮式机器人在复杂地形（如连续楼梯）上的环境编码能力、运动平滑性与地形适应性。

Abstract: Recent advances in control have enabled bipedal-wheeled robots to traverse slopes and single-step obstacles, yet long staircase traversal remains challenging as current teacher-student frameworks suffer from weakened dynamics-aware representations and incomplete terrain geometry encoding. To bridge this gap, we propose DynaWM, a dynamics-aware representation learning framework. To enhance terrain encoding capability and enable transparent assessment, we introduce a world model as a regularizer to enforce forward-dynamics awareness, preserving comprehensive terrain geometry while facilitating hierarchical encoding visualization. To stabilize knowledge transfer, we employ a momentum target encoder to provide consistent distillation targets, preventing dimensional collapse from non-stationary teacher updates. Evaluation of the learned representations through Principal Component Analysis (PCA) visualization and quantitative metrics reveals that our encoder hierarchically captures terrain geometry with higher terrain encoding capability, leading to enhanced terrain adaptability and motion smoothness. Experimental results in simulation and real hardware demonstrate that our method achieves superior terrain adaptability and motion smoothness, enabling bipedal-wheeled robots to overcome diverse continuous stairs, as shown in Fig. 1.

</details>


### [158] [NavWM: A Unified Navigation World Model for Foresight-Driven Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24101)
*Yanghong Mei,Longteng Guo,Ming-Ming Yu,Guiyu Zhao,Xingjian He,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavWM 是一种统一的导航世界模型，通过潜空间表示和多模态轨迹生成，解决了传统方法在复杂环境中决策短视和动作单一的问题，实现了更鲁棒的闭环导航规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉导航策略存在“近视”决策和模式崩溃问题，且现有的范式将感知、生成和控制孤立处理，无法有效捕捉时空动态特性。

Method: 提出 NavWM，通过潜空间世界标记（latent world tokens）提取几何与语义先验，并引入基于锚点（anchor-based）的多模态轨迹预测框架来生成多样化动作空间，实现闭环规划与视觉预测。

Result: 在多个机器人实验数据集上实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能，在高质量未来状态生成和零样本（zero-shot）导航成功率方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: NavWM 通过统一的架构成功解决了视觉导航中缺乏长远规划和动作模式单一的问题。该方法证明了在统一框架内结合世界模型建模与多模态轨迹预测，能显著增强机器人在复杂环境下的导航能力和泛化性。

Abstract: Conventional visual navigation policies often struggle with myopic decision-making and mode collapse in complex environments. While world models offer a promising alternative, existing paradigms typically isolate perception, generation, and control, failing to capture their shared spatio-temporal dynamics. In this paper, we propose NavWM, a unified navigation world model that seamlessly integrates latent world reasoning, multimodal action prediction, and controllable visual generation. At its core, NavWM leverages latent world tokens to distill geometric and semantic priors, endowing the agent with robust structural understanding. To overcome the limitations of deterministic policies, we introduce an anchor-based multimodal trajectory forecasting framework that generates a diverse action space. This inherent diversity explicitly empowers the generative world model to act as a robust closed-loop planner, utilizing visual foresight to evaluate and select the optimal path. Extensive experiments across diverse robotics datasets demonstrate that NavWM significantly advances the state-of-the-art, delivering remarkable improvements in both high-fidelity future state generation and zero-shot navigation success.

</details>


### [159] [The Evaluation Cost of Task Specialization in Evolutionary Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24191)
*Paolo Leopardi,Heiko Hamann,Jonas Kuckling,Tanja Katharina Kaiser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过成本效益分析证明，在大规模多机器人系统中，通过将演化评估预算进行适当分配，更容易（且成本更低）地演化出优于通才的专业化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有研究认为任务专业化能提高多机器人系统效率，但通才型控制器在演化评估预算分配上具有天然优势（无需分割预算），因此需要定量评估“专家型 vs 通才型”在资源消耗上的成本效益权衡。

Method: 在基于物理的机器人仿真器中，通过演化优化方法对觅食任务进行实验，对比分析了从零开始演化任务专家型控制器与演化通才型控制器所需的“总评估预算（total evaluation budget）”。

Result: 研究发现，随着多机器人系统规模的增加，演化出“专家型”行为并使其性能超越“通才型”行为所需的总评估预算随之下降。

Conclusion: 随着多机器人系统（MRS）规模的扩大，任务专家型控制器在演化过程中所需的资源投入（评估预算）呈现出相对于通才型控制器的优势，即只需更少的总预算即可演化出超越通才的表现。

Abstract: Task specialization can improve the efficiency of multi-robot systems (MRSs). Previous works have investigated the emergence of task-specialist robot controllers through evolutionary optimization and have argued that task specialization is more likely to evolve when subtask behaviors are readily available as building blocks. However, the available evaluation budget must be distributed across all subtasks, whereas a single generalist behavior can exploit the entire budget for its own optimization. We present a cost-benefit analysis of evolving task-specialist versus generalist behaviors in a foraging scenario here. In a physics-based robotics simulator, we study the total evaluation budget required to evolve task-specialist behaviors that outperform generalist behaviors across MRS sizes. We show that with increasing MRS size, a lower total evaluation budget is sufficient to evolve specialists that outperform generalists.

</details>


### [160] [Grounding Generative Policies in Physics: Optimization-Guided Diffusion for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24208)
*Sabrina Bodmer,René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Hao Ma,Alexandre Didier,Marco Hutter,Colin Jones,Melanie Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带物理约束的扩散模型推理引导框架，有效解决机器人任务规划中的可行性难题，提升了任务成功率，无需重训模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然能生成高质量分布数据，但直接应用在机器人上常因忽视物理约束（如避障、运动学可达性、控制稳定性）而导致失效；现有的无约束生成难以弥合不同机器人具身的“具身鸿沟”。

Method: 提出一种推理时（Inference-time）优化框架，将扩散模型的去噪过程表述为带约束的优化问题，通过“优化修正（optimized correction）”替代标准的采样扰动，强制满足硬约束或软惩罚条件。

Result: 在灵巧手抓取和动态操作任务中，该方法相比投影法或梯度引导基线，在满足物理约束的同时更好地保留了生成质量；任务成功率分别提升了20个百分点和23个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法通过推理时的约束优化，成功在不重新训练扩散模型的前提下，使生成结果同时满足物理可执行性约束与分布先验要求，有效解决了机器人任务规划中的“具身鸿沟”问题。

Abstract: Diffusion models sample effectively from high-dimensional, multimodal distributions, but their outputs may violate deployment constraints. For task-space robot policies, generated grasps, waypoints, or trajectories can be distributionally valid yet infeasible, violating reachability, collision-avoidance, or closed-loop executability requirements. This embodiment gap limits zero-shot deployment across robots, even when the task-space behavior itself is transferable.
  We propose an inference-time optimization framework that couples the behavior generation to physical feasibility by formulating diffusion guidance as a constrained optimization problem. Our key insight is to replace the sampling perturbation in the backward process with an optimized correction, allowing hard constraints or soft penalties to be imposed during sampling without the need to retrain the diffusion model, while keeping samples close to the learned prior.
  We evaluate the method on dexterous grasp synthesis with reachability and collision-avoidance constraints, and dynamic manipulation with controller-level trackability constraints. Across settings and robot embodiments, optimization-guided denoising matches the feasibility of projection- and gradient-guidance baselines while better preserving grasp quality, and improving controller-level executability and task success, with task success improving by up to 20pp. on dexterous grasping and 23pp. on visuomotor manipulation over the best baseline.

</details>


### [161] [RoBoSR: Structured Scene Representations for Embodied Robotic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24338)
*Kewei Hu,Wanchan Yu,Fangwen Chen,Jing Jiajian,Zimeng Li,Ying Wei,Tianhao Liu,Michael Zhang,Hanwen Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对具身智能在复杂场景下推理能力不足的问题，本文提出RoBoSR中间表示，通过结构化的场景图建模实现长时程任务的因果推理，并配合大规模数据集实现显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能方法过度依赖示范驱动的序列偏置，导致其在应对开放式和长时程任务时灵活性不足，且缺乏对演化状态的结构化推理能力。

Method: 1. 提出RoBoSR（中间结构化表示）：将操作任务建模为基于语义对齐、以物体为中心的场景图的逐步状态转移。2. 构建数据集Manip-Cognition-1.6M：用于联合监督场景理解、指令解读和子任务规划。3. 在知觉-动作接口处建模物体状态与空间关系，实现高层推理与原始输入的解耦。

Result: 在多个基准测试及真实世界演示中，RoBoSR在零样本泛化和长时程任务表现上均优于基于提示（prompting）的方法和传统TAMP基线。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示（如RoBoSR）是提升具身智能在开放式、长时程任务中可扩展推理的关键归纳偏置。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, embodied reasoning under real-world variability remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on demonstration-driven sequential biases, limiting flexibility in open-ended and long-horizon tasks that require structured reasoning over evolving states.
  We introduce RoBoSR, an intermediate structural representation that formulates manipulation as step-wise state transitions over semantically grounded, object-centric scene graphs. By modeling object states and their spatial relations at the perception-action interface, RoBoSR disentangles high-level task reasoning from raw inputs and enables structured reasoning over preconditions, effects, and goal states. This representation endows the agent with causal reasoning capability, enforcing subtask dependencies and supporting coherent long-horizon task planning.
  To learn such structure-aware reasoning, we construct Manip-Cognition-1.6M, an open-world dataset that jointly supervises scene understanding, instruction interpretation, and subtask planning across diverse tasks.
  Across several benchmarks and real-world demonstrations, our method consistently outperforms prompting-based methods and classical TAMP baselines in zero-shot generalization and long-horizon tasks. The results underscore structured intermediate representations as a critical inductive bias for scalable embodied reasoning.

</details>


### [162] [SlipSense: Multimodal Sensing for Online Slip Detection in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24350)
*Iris Szu-Yao Liu,Chien Chern Cheah,Meng Yee Michael Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 SlipSense 的基于力的滑移检测框架，通过配备定制化传感足的四足机器人，利用 LSTM 模型实现对早期滑移的高精度在线检测，显著优于传统运动学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动学和本体感受的滑移检测方法在检测早期滑移时灵敏度不足，无法有效防止导致不稳定的灾难性滑移。

Method: 提出 SlipSense 框架，采用定制的轻量化传感足，融合多模态传感器数据，利用基于 LSTM 的模型来推断地面反作用力（GRF）并识别滑移异常。

Result: 在 Unitree Go1 四足机器人上实现盲态实时滑移检测；滑移检测平均精度达 85.9%，滑移位移检测灵敏度达到 24.1±6.4mm，相比基准方案精度提升 24%，检测分辨率提升约 3.3 倍。

Conclusion: SlipSense 通过基于力的定制化传感足实现了对四足机器人早期滑移的实时、高精度检测，优于传统运动学方法，为未来实现基于力的步态适应与增强稳定性提供了基础。

Abstract: Legged robots rely on accurate ground interaction awareness to traverse variable terrains, such as slippery surfaces. Existing slip detection methods often rely on kinematics and proprioception, which lack the sensitivity to detect early-stage slips that occur prior to catastrophic instability. Thus, this paper presents SlipSense, a novel framework for online force-based slip detection using a custom lightweight sensorized foot for quadrupeds to detect slip. The framework integrates a multimodal sensor design with a LSTM-based model to infer ground reaction forces and detect slip-indicative anomalies during locomotion. The proposed framework is deployed on a Unitree Go1 quadruped to demonstrate blind online slip detection over a slippery terrain. Our method detects early-stage slips down to an average displacement of 24.1 +/-6.4mm with an overall accuracy of 85.9%. This represents a 3.3-fold finer detection resolution and a 24% relative accuracy improvement over a standard kinematic baseline that uses foot velocity inferred through state estimation. The work in this paper serves as a foundation for force-aware gait adaptation in legged robotic locomotion, allowing future controllers to estimate terrain friction and adjust constraints, thus improving the overall stability of the system.

</details>


### [163] [RE4: Transformation-aware Imitation of Object Interactions Using Manipulation Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24403)
*Arsh Chawla,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RE4框架，通过结合自监督位姿估计与模式感知的演示检索与变换，在对象操作模仿学习中平衡了高性能与系统可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端模仿学习方法（如扩散模型和流模型）在性能上取得了显著提升，但牺牲了系统的可解释性。研究旨在构建一个既能利用现有先进基准，又能保持操纵理论可解释性的框架。

Method: 提出RE4框架，包含四个核心步骤：1) 利用演示数据进行轻量级自监督位姿估计；2) 基于操作模式感知进行演示检索；3) 模式感知的演示变换；4) 满足模式约束的重规划与部署（Rollout）。

Result: 在Push-T和Robomimic（基于状态和图像）基准测试中表现良好，特别是在图像基准的稀疏数据区域表现出更强的鲁棒性，并在低数据量环境下具有优势。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过结合经典的操纵理论与轻量化自监督位姿估计，可以在保持高性能的同时，通过可解释的模块化组件实现复杂的对象交互任务。

Abstract: Object interaction tasks have been a focus of advances in imitation learning. End-to-end methods, dominated by diffusion and flow-based variants have shown leaps in performance while sacrificing interpretability. Object-centric and pose-informed variants have had a role in learning from demonstration in manipulation tasks. In this paper, we revisit a few modern imitation learning benchmarks for object interactions, with the aim of composing a framework that repurposes principled theories of manipulation, preserving both performance and interpretability. For image observations, lightweight training is proposed for model-free pose estimation of the target object, using self-supervision over the demonstration data available for imitation learning. This information is then used to inform a manipulation mode-aware retrieval of a demonstration, a mode-aware transformation, a replan step that connects to the retrieval point while preserving mode constraints, and finally rolling out the transformed demonstration. These compose four key steps of the proposed RE4 framework, evaluated over state-based and image-based benchmarks in Push-T and Robomimic. An adversarial benchmark that evaluates sparse data regions of image-based Push-T showcases the robustness, further bolstered by indications from low-data regime experiments. The current work shows promise in using simple interpretable building blocks to learn manipulation skills.

</details>


### [164] [Legible and Intuitive Multi-modal Robot State and Intent Communication Validated in Online and Real-world Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24445)
*Tim Schreiter,Jens V. Rüppel,Andrey Rudenko,Martin Magnusson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究对比了移动机器人的单模态（LED）与多模态（凝视/手势/语音）沟通策略，证实了多模态更直观，并揭示了虚拟实验结果在真实物理场景中存在显著的易读性衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与人类协作效率依赖于高效的沟通，但现有研究在机器人模态受限、人类解释差异性以及虚拟环境与真实物理环境下的沟通差异（虚拟到现实差距）方面探索不足，需要验证现有工业机器人沟通策略的有效性。

Method: 采用系统性的大规模对比验证方法，对比了“低表现力的单模态LED”与“高表现力的多模态（凝视、手势、语音）”两种策略；实验设置涵盖了在线虚拟实验与线下实地实验，覆盖了转弯意图、请求注意、错误状态、被困状态及正常工作五种消息类型。

Result: 多模态交互在易读性和直观性上表现更强；研究发现从在线实验到真实世界评估，整体的易读性明显下降，尤其是LED信号的传递效果，用户在真实环境中的消息理解置信度也会降低。

Conclusion: 高度表现力的多模态（凝视、手势、语音）交流方式显著优于单一模态的LED交流；研究强调了“虚拟到现实”之间的差距，即在实验室/在线环境下的实验结果往往会高估在现实环境中的交流易读性和置信度。

Abstract: Effective robot-to-human communication can increase transparency and trust, reduce uncertainty, and contribute to safer collaboration in shared workspaces. Designing and validating an effective robot communication strategy is challenging due to the varying and often limited communication modalities across robots, differences in how diverse recipients interpret messages, and the underexplored virtual-to-real gap in studies of communication legibility. We present a systematic, large-scale comparative validation of existing communication strategies for a mobile non-humanoid robot across message types and settings (online and in-person). Based on the prescribed message types in the existing standards for industrial robots, we realize and compare a low-expressive, unimodal LED-based strategy with a highly expressive, multimodal one that leverages robotic gaze, gestures, and voice. For each strategy, we analyze the communication of a turning intention, an attention request, error status, whether the robot is stuck, and whether it is functioning normally. We evaluate these strategies in replicated online and in-person experiments. We find strong evidence that highly expressive multimodal communication is perceived as more legible and intuitive than unimodal LED-based communication. Comparing the online and real-world study findings, we observe a notable decrease in overall legibility, particularly for signaling with LEDs. Similarly, confidence in message interpretation decreases during the real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [Supervise What Survives: Geometry-Guided VLA Adaptation from Synthetic Robot Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24448)
*Danze Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Qiming Huang,Zhijun Cao,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决生成视频在机器人训练中因动作缺失导致的伪影问题，GRA提出仅从生成视频中提取空间几何作为几何锚点辅助视觉表征，而动作学习完全依赖真实数据，从而实现更鲁棒的机器人策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出用生成的视频数据进行动作预测存在“不对称保留”问题：生成的视频保留了任务的几何空间信息（where），但丢失了实际的控制信号（how），导致将生成的视频直接视作动作数据存在抽象偏差。

Method: 提出GRA（几何引导的表征对齐）方法：1. 不使用生成的视频拟合动作，仅利用其提取2D末端执行器航点（waypoints）；2. 通过pose estimation、重定向和投影将几何信息提取为空间锚点；3. 在VLA模型中引入辅助2D头进行对齐，动作头仅由真实机器人数据训练。

Result: 在真实机器人任务中，GRA的表现优于现有的伪动作（pseudo-action）基准方法，并能显著缩小由于真实数据不足带来的性能差距。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了“不对称保留原则”，证实利用生成的视频提取几何信息辅助视觉感知，比强行拟合伪动作更有效地提升了机器人策略的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models require large-scale video-action pairs, yet real teleoperation remains scarce. While generated robot videos offer a scalable alternative, existing methods treat them as real robot data by recovering pseudo-actions from synthesized pixels. We argue that deriving low-level control from generated visuals is a mismatched abstraction. A video captures only \emph{geometry}: the spatial trajectory representing the \emph{where} of a task. A real demonstration captures \emph{control}: the exact motor commands representing the \emph{how}. Human-to-robot video generation preserves these unequally: the visible geometry survives the generation process, while the underlying control signals are lost. This \textbf{Asymmetric Preservation Principle} dictates a clean rule: this surviving geometry should solely supervise visual perception, leaving control to real demonstrations. Following this principle, we propose \textbf{GRA} (\textbf{G}eometry-guided \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}lignment), which extracts the geometric content as future 2D end-effector waypoints, computed from the source human video through pose estimation, retargeting, simulation, and calibrated projection, and routes them to the VLA vision backbone via an auxiliary 2D head. The action head is trained on real demonstrations only. During fine-tuning, the waypoint loss persists as a \textbf{spatial representation anchor} that prevents the backbone from losing its geometric grounding. On real-robot tasks, GRA outperforms pseudo-action baselines under matched data budgets and narrows the gap to policies trained with substantially more real demonstrations, suggesting that correctly routed geometry bridges generated videos to robot policies more reliably than recovered actions.

</details>


### [166] [NoContactNoWorries: Estimating Contact through Vision and Proprioception for In-Hand Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24450)
*Soham Patil,Avirup Das,Sourabh Bhosale,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NoContactNoWorries 提出了一种利用 Transformer 融合 RGB-D 视觉与本体感受来推断触觉接触状态的框架，作为昂贵实体触觉传感器的替代方案，显著提升了机器人复杂操作任务的可扩展性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人精细操作需感知物理接触，但传统专用触觉传感器存在成本高、易损、集成困难等问题。研究旨在探索如何模仿人类利用视觉和本体感受推断接触的能力，以提供一种可扩展的、基于视觉的替代触觉感知方案。

Method: 提出了名为 NoContactNoWorries 的基于 Transformer 的多模态框架。该方法将 RGB-D 视觉信息与机器人本体感受（proprioception）进行融合，训练模型预测二值化（binary）的接触状态，并将此预测结果作为“伪触觉（pseudo-tactile）”信号应用于下游任务。

Result: 模型在多个物体上表现出良好的训练效果，并在手内物体重新定向（in-hand object reorientation）任务的强化学习中验证了其有效性；实验证明该方法具有良好的泛化能力，能够推广至未见过的物体，并在仿真及真实机器人实验中验证了其可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了仅依靠视觉和本体感受推断机器人接触状态的可行性，为实现更具泛化能力和鲁棒性的机器人操作提供了一种高效的替代性感知方案，降低了对昂贵且脆弱的硬件触觉传感器的依赖。

Abstract: Perceiving physical contact is fundamental to dexterous manipulation. While robots often rely on dedicated hardware tactile sensors, humans exhibit a remarkable ability to infer contact by integrating visual information with an innate sense of their body's pose and movement. Inspired by this embodied perceptual skill, we investigate whether a robot can learn to infer contact from vision, an approach that also offers a scalable alternative to tactile hardware specifically for binary contact estimation, which faces practical challenges in cost, fragility, and integration. We present NoContactNoWorries, a transformer-based multimodal framework that fuses RGB-D vision with the robot's proprioception to infer binary contact states as a pseudo-tactile signal for hand-object interactions. We validate by training a single contact prediction model on multiple objects and show that the inferred contact signal supports downstream reinforcement learning agents for in-hand object reorientation, generalizing to novel objects. Experiments in both simulation and on a real-world robot validate our approach, highlighting the feasibility of inferring contact from vision and proprioception. Project Page: https://soham2560.github.io/no-contact-no-worries/

</details>


### [167] [Varying Bundle Size Reactive Multi-Task Assignment using Selective Cost Estimation for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24462)
*Niklas Dahlquist,Shridhar Velhal,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多机器人任务分配中的计算瓶颈，提出了一种混合多保真度捆绑生成策略，通过结合快速低保真搜索与精细高保真验证，实现了实时、高效且保护隐私的任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂环境中，传统的组合拍卖式任务分配因任务执行成本评估计算量大，且捆绑生成具有指数级复杂度，难以满足实时反应式应用的需求。

Method: 提出一种分布式、两阶段的多保真度（multi-fidelity）捆绑生成方法：1. 智能体利用低保真启发式算法（如欧几里得距离）进行快速搜索；2. 针对高潜力候选方案利用高保真路径规划进行精细化评估；3. 最后由中央协调器解决集合包装（set packing）问题以确保全局可行性和效用最大化。

Result: 在多种环境下的仿真实验表明，该框架有效提升了反应式基于拍卖的任务分配性能，支持动态改变捆绑规模，并实现了智能体状态与内部成本模型的隐私保护。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了在保持计算可行性的同时，进行高效且具备反应能力的多机器人任务分配，且能够保护智能体的隐私。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable framework for multi-robot task allocation in complex environments where estimating task execution costs is computationally expensive. While combinatorial auction-based approaches offer reliable solutions, the exponential complexity of bundle generation typically renders them intractable for real-time reactive applications, particularly when accurate path planning is required for cost validation. We address this through a distributed, two-stage multi-fidelity bundle generation approach. Agents utilize a local search tree guided by a low-fidelity heuristic (such as euclidean distance) to rapidly explore the bundle space, applying high-fidelity path planning only to the most promising candidates in a best-first manner. These refined bids are then submitted to a central coordinator that solves a set packing problem to ensure global feasibility and maximize the overall utility. Simulation results in multiple environments demonstrate that the framework is able to improve the performance of reactive auction-based task allocation. Overall, the presented framework is shown to enable reactive task allocation with dynamic bundle sizes in multiple settings without exposing the agents' state and internal cost estimation models.

</details>


### [168] [FT-WBC: Learning Fault-Tolerant Whole-Body Control for Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24466)
*Yudong Zhong,Pengfei Mai,Sikai Guo,Jiahang Cao,Zhihai Bi,Qiuyue Liu,Ziyan Feng,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对足式机械臂在故障下的稳定性与操作能力缺失问题，本文提出了 FT-WBC 框架，通过故障感知与姿态自适应机制，实现稳健的全身容错控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机械臂在执行器故障时，机械臂导致的重心偏移与动态扰动极易引发系统失稳；且现有容错控制多局限于足式移动，缺乏针对 loco-manipulation 的全身稳定性与工作空间权衡方案。

Method: 提出 FT-WBC 框架，采用解耦的上下半身策略架构，包含故障估计器（FE）用于识别故障关节，以及姿态适配模块（PAM）用于实时调整基座姿态，以补偿执行器故障并动态分配任务优先级。

Result: 在仿真及实机实验中验证了该框架能显著提高系统在执行器衰减或锁死故障下的生存率，并在保持全身稳定的同时最大化了机械臂的工作空间，具备零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: FT-WBC 框架有效解决了足式机械臂在执行器故障下的全身稳定性与操作空间保持问题，实现了在仿真和现实环境中的稳健容错控制。

Abstract: Legged manipulators combine the mobility of legged platforms with the manipulation capability of robotic arms. However, arm-induced Center-of-Mass shifts and dynamic disturbances make the system more prone to instability under actuator failures, potentially leading to falls, task failures, or safety risks. Existing fault-tolerant control methods mainly focus on locomotion alone, leaving the coupled problem of whole-body stability and arm reachability in fault-tolerant loco-manipulation largely unaddressed. To bridge this gap, we propose FT-WBC, a fault-tolerant loco-manipulation framework for robust whole-body control of legged manipulators under actuator failures. FT-WBC adopts a decoupled upper- and lower-body policy architecture and introduces two key modules: a Fault Estimator (FE) and a Posture Adaptation Module (PAM). The FE predicts faulty joints from lower-body proprioceptive histories, while the PAM uses this fault information to adapt the base posture plan generated by the arm policy, converting potentially unstable posture requests into safe and executable base posture commands. Through this fault-aware posture adaptation mechanism, FT-WBC synthesizes compensatory gaits under actuator failures and preserves as much arm workspace as possible while maintaining whole-body stability. Simulation and real-world experiments show that FT-WBC significantly improves survival rate and workspace under weakening or locked failures, and transfers zero-shot to a real legged manipulator in the real world.

</details>


### [169] [G$^3$VLA: Geometric inductive bias for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24472)
*Yue Peng,Yongzhe Zhao,Artur Habuda,Khuyen Pham,Yanheng Zhu,Tran Nguyen Le,Fares Abu-Dakka,Li Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G$^3$VLA 模块通过注入校准的相机几何信息（无需深度标注），提升了 VLA 模型在多视角机器人操作中的几何推理与执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型处理多视角输入时，视觉标记仅限于 2D 坐标，缺乏对机器人相机参数的几何感知，导致空间推理能力不足。

Method: 提出 G$^3$VLA 模块，包含：(1) 内参条件化的射线嵌入；(2) 投影位置编码 (PRoPE)；(3) 双向跨视图融合。利用预训练模型或置信度门控的 $\pi^3$X 教师模型进行几何监督，无需深度传感器或手动标注。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa24、RoboTwin2.0 及真实机器人实验中取得显著性能提升，特别是在空间细粒度和对象敏感任务中表现突出，且验证了其对 $\pi_0$、$\pi_{0.5}$ 及 GR00T 1.5 等多种架构的有效性。

Conclusion: G$^3$VLA 通过在 VLA 模型中注入几何先验，有效解决了多视角操作任务中的空间对齐与几何感知不足问题，且具有良好的跨模型和任务泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made rapid progress in generalist robot manipulation by harnessing semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language backbones, but their visual tokens remain grounded in 2D image coordinates rather than the calibrated geometry of the robot's cameras -- a mismatch especially pronounced in multi-camera setups, where views are coupled by known intrinsics and extrinsics yet processed as independent images. We propose G$^3$VLA, a camera-aware geometric module that injects calibrated structure into the visual-token stream of a pretrained VLA without altering its action space or imitation objective, combining intrinsic-conditioned ray embeddings, projective positional encoding (PRoPE), and bidirectional cross-view fusion. Geometric supervision is provided either from ground-truth point maps when available, or from confidence-gated $π^3$X teacher predictions, requiring no depth sensors or manual annotations. Instantiated on $π_0$, G$^3$VLA yields consistent gains across the LIBERO suites, RoboCasa24, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot settings, with the largest improvements on spatially and object-sensitive tasks. We further validate on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T 1.5, with results suggesting that geometric transfer is most effective when geometry-aware tokens have direct access to the action generation pathway. Our project page is at https://sites.google.com/view/g3vla

</details>


### [170] [Decentralized Pose Graph Riemannian Optimization for Object-based Multi-Robot SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24489)
*Yixian Zhao,Yan Huang,Yang Xu,Liang Li,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多机器人对象SLAM的去中心化姿态图优化问题，提出了一种基于黎曼流形的分布式近似牛顿优化框架，有效解决了通信受限环境下的协同估计，提升了计算效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对象基多机器人SLAM去中心化解法通常假设通信拓扑与物理交互拓扑高度一致，而在现实中通信往往稀疏、间歇或时变，限制了系统的适用性。

Method: 1. 提出了一种基于黎曼流形优化的去中心化算法，通过共识机制解耦轨迹与对象位姿的联合估计问题；2. 设计了一种分布式近似牛顿法，利用局部二阶信息在SE(d)流形上直接优化；3. 进行了收敛性分析及条件数分析，证明了该方法在减少迭代次数和通信开销方面的优势。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著降低了迭代次数和通信开销，同时保持了估计精度。在公开基准集、大规模仿真及实机实验中均表现出优异的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于黎曼优化和分布式近似牛顿法的去中心化框架，在多机器人对象SLAM中实现了更高的估计精度、计算效率、网络拓扑可扩展性及对通信故障的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pose graph optimization (PGO) is a key back-end component for state estimation in networked multi-robot simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). In object-based multi-robot SLAM, the problem becomes more tightly coupled because robots must jointly estimate both their trajectories and the poses of persistent objects observed by multiple agents. Existing decentralized solutions often assume that the communication graph closely matches the physical interaction topology, which is restrictive in realistic deployments where communication is sparse, intermittent, or time-varying. This paper presents a fully decentralized Riemannian optimization framework for object-based multi-robot PGO that decouples the coupled estimation problem via a consensus mechanism, enabling flexible communication topologies. To improve convergence under limited communication budgets, we further develop a distributed approximate-Newton scheme that exploits local second-order information while operating directly on the SE(d) manifold to preserve geometric consistency, and we establish the convergence to Riemannian first-order stationary points and provide a local condition-number analysis explaining the benefit of approximate second-order information over first-order Riemannian descent. The resulting method reduces iteration count and communication overhead without sacrificing estimation accuracy. Extensive evaluations on public benchmarks, large-scale simulations, and real-world multi-robot experiments demonstrate improved accuracy, runtime efficiency, scalability across network topologies, and robustness to communication failures.

</details>


### [171] [Explaining Failures of Cyber-Physical Systems with Actual Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24546)
*Khen Elimelech,Tom Yaacov,David A. Kelly,Hana Chockler,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶等复杂CPS系统难以解释“黑盒”故障的问题，本文引入实际因果关系概念，提出了两套兼顾性能与效率的故障解释算法，实现了对复杂系统异常行为的因果归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自动驾驶等CPS系统日益复杂，且因包含神经网络组件常呈现“黑盒”特性，难以实现完全形式化验证。系统在其规格说明失效时（即发生故障）往往伴随灾难性后果，本研究旨在通过因果推理技术，为CPS的意外行为提供可解释性分析，以提升系统可靠性并指导故障缓解。

Method: 1. 填补了实际因果关系框架在CPS领域应用中的理论空白；2. 提出了两种针对CPS的系统分析无关（system-agnostic）解释衍生算法，分别针对“解释最优性”和“推导效率”进行权衡优化。

Result: 在自动驾驶汽车避障场景中进行了实验验证，证明了所提方法能够有效地解析神经网络控制器的故障原因，并平衡了计算复杂度和解释质量。

Conclusion: 该论文首次将实际因果关系理论应用于CPS故障解释，成功架起了复杂系统故障诊断与因果推理之间的理论桥梁，并提供了可实际落地的算法实现，有效提升了黑盒CPS的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: Modern autonomous Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), such as self-driving cars, face increasingly complex demands, and yet are expected to act reliably. The black-box nature often characterizing such systems, especially those relying on neural components, makes it impossible to fully verify the system behavior prior to deployment. Unfortunately, unexpected failures-when the system does not comply with its specification-are inevitable and may have catastrophic implications. To improve trust in the system and facilitate future mitigation after a failure occurs, it is important to try to derive an explanation for the unexpected system behavior. This paper introduces the novel concept of leveraging the framework of actual causality for CPS failure explanation. Up until now, this framework was only used to derive explanations in the context of simple systems, such as image classifiers. This paper addresses the theoretical gaps and provides the guidance needed to allow for correct explanation derivation in the CPS domain. Beyond the theoretical contribution, the paper presents two novel, practical, system-agnostic explanation derivation algorithms, allowing to prioritize either explanation optimality or derivation efficiency. The approach is demonstrated and evaluated in the context of a neural-network-controlled autonomous car, designed to avoid collisions.

</details>


### [172] [Enabling Robust Cloth Manipulation via Inference-Time Simulator-in-the-Loop Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24552)
*Xin Liu,Yulin Li,Ziming Li,Pengyu Jing,Zhenhao Huang,Bingyang Zhou,Ziqiu Zeng,Siyuan Luo,Chenkun Qi,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用单目RGB视频流进行布料操纵的在线优化框架，通过高效的FLASH物理仿真器与合成数据驱动的视觉映射模块，实现了在复杂变形体操作中的实时轨迹规划与闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决布料操纵（deformable-object manipulation）中由于状态空间复杂、接触计算困难而导致的在线规划难问题，将原本在刚体操作中行之有效的“Simulator-in-the-loop（模拟器循环采样优化）”范式扩展至布料操纵领域。

Method: 1. 使用FLASH模拟器构建高效、稳定的布料物理引擎（支持大规模并行滚动采样）；2. 开发实时到模拟（Real-to-Sim）视觉映射模块，仅通过合成数据训练，结合预训练视觉特征与可学习的标准Token将单目RGB转化为模拟环境中的布料状态；3. 采用基于先验引导的MPPI（模型预测路径积分）算法，结合稀疏网格滚动后端，围绕离线提炼的专家策略进行在线优化规划。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，该方法相比基准方法实现了更高的任务成功率，并表现出更强的环境鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将基于模拟器的在线规划（Simulator-in-the-loop）应用于复杂动态的布料操纵是可行的，通过合成数据驱动的视觉观测映射与高效的物理仿真模型，有效提升了单目视觉布料操作的成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Simulator-in-the-loop optimization offers a promising inference-time mechanism for robot manipulation. It uses a physical simulator as a backend rollout engine to evaluate candidate trajectories in parallel and refine nominal actions online, a paradigm proven effective in rigid-body manipulation where state and contact are relatively tractable. We bring this paradigm to real-world cloth manipulation from a single RGB input through three pillars. (i) We design a scalable synthetic-data generation and inference-time rollout pipeline built on FLASH, a deformable-object simulator that provides a practical balance among physical fidelity, numerical stability, and rollout efficiency. (ii) We develop a real-to-sim module, trained purely on synthetic data, that maps a single RGB observation to simulation-compatible cloth state by fusing pretrained visual features with learnable canonical tokens. (iii) We perform online planning by coupling a sparse-mesh rollout backend with prior-guided MPPI, anchored at an offline-distilled policy trajectory, preserving manipulation-relevant deformation and contact while enabling sufficient parallel rollout batches. Real-robot experiments show higher success rates and stronger robustness than baseline methods.

</details>


### [173] [ArtiTwinSplat: Interactable Digital Twin Reconstruction via Gaussian Splatting from RGB-D videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24628)
*Pranjal Mishra,René Zurbrügg,Max Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArtiTwinSplat 是一种无需人工标注或 CAD 模型即可直接从真实 RGB-D 视频中构建具有关节属性、高保真数字孪生模型的新框架，旨在解决机器人操作非结构化环境物体时的部署难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化真实环境中部署机器人需要精确的交互式物体模型，但目前在大规模构建这些模型方面存在瓶颈，现有方法多局限于仿真环境，难以直接应用于现实场景。

Method: 1. 基于 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 技术保持几何保真度和光度真实感；2. 采用无监督关节发现流水线 (Unsupervised articulation discovery pipeline)，仅从观察到的运动中恢复部件结构和关节运动学；3. 结合跟踪与优化阶段以确保模型的稳定性和可查询性。

Result: 该方法能够构建出支持实时渲染、视点控制和交互式操作的数字孪生体，无需 CAD 模型、仿真资产或人工标注，并能直接服务于机器人规划与学习系统。

Conclusion: ArtiTwinSplat 提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，能够直接从真实世界的 RGB-D 视频中构建可交互的数字孪生模型，降低了机器人进行关节物体操作的门槛，促进了具身智能的发展。

Abstract: Deploying robots in unstructured real-world environments needs accurate, interactive models of the objects. Constructing these models at scale remains a critical bottleneck for robotic system integration. We present ArtiTwinSplat, a framework that automatically constructs articulated, photo-realistic digital twins of objects directly from RGB-D videos, requiring no CAD models, simulation assets, or manual annotations. Our method is built on 3D Gaussian Splatting that preserve geometric fidelity and photometric realism, coupled with an unsupervised articulation discovery pipeline that recovers part structure and joint kinematics from observed motion alone. With tracking and optimization stages our method provides stable, queryable digital twins that support real-time rendering, viewpoint control, and interactive manipulation. Unlike prior methods confined to simulation, ArtiTwinSplat operates directly on real-world observations and produces twins that are immediately usable by downstream robot planning and learning systems. This method offers a practical, scalable pathway toward digital twin construction, lowering the integration barrier for articulated object manipulation in embodied AI and human-robot collaboration contexts.

</details>


### [174] [Optimization-based Safe Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Ground Vehicle in Multi-Floor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24631)
*Zishang Xiang,Runda Zhang,Runqi Chai,Kaiyuan Chen,Senchun Chai,Yuanqing Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多层自动驾驶场景的分层轨迹规划框架，通过GVD任务规划与热启动优化轨迹生成，实现了复杂环境下的快速、高质量路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自动驾驶车辆（AGV）在多层复杂场景中缺乏高效、系统的轨迹规划解决方案，旨在解决多楼层环境下的路径优化与约束处理难题。

Method: 1. 任务规划模块：引入广义沃罗诺伊图（GVD）与多目标算法进行楼层出口的战略选择；2. 轨迹规划模块：采用基于优化的方法并设计热启动分层规划框架以加速收敛；3. 约束处理：提出了一种相关性约束计算方法，以简化复杂环境下的障碍物约束。

Result: 仿真实验证实，该框架在规划效率、收敛速度及复杂障碍物规避方面均表现出良好的性能，验证了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多层场景轨迹规划框架有效且可行，能够显著提升自动驾驶车辆在复杂多层环境下的路径规划效率与质量。

Abstract: The development of trajectory planning strategies for autonomous ground vehicles (AGVs) represents a prevailing research interest within the domain of intelligent transportation systems. This paper introduces a trajectory planning framework tailored for multi-floor scenarios. The framework consists of two main modules: the task planning module and the trajectory planning module. The task planning module involves a strategic selection phase, where a task planning strategy based on generalized voronoi diagrams (GVD) and multi-objective algorithms is proposed to select the floor exits for each floor. The trajectory planning module utilizes optimization-based methods to generate high-quality trajectories, and a warm-started hierarchical planning framework is designed to ensure rapid convergence. Additionally, for handling complex obstacle constraints, a correlation constraint calculation method is designed for reducing obstacle constraints in trajectory planning. Finally, the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed framework are verified through simulations.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Monotonic Progress: Retry-Supervised Value Learning for Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24633)
*Xinyao Qin,Junjie Lu,Kaixin Wang,Chuheng Zhang,Sinjae Kang,Kimin Lee,Min Xu,Bin Liang,Jun Yang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReTVL 框架通过引入“重试”事件来监督值函数学习，有效识别并处理机器人离线演示中的失误与修正过程，从而提升模仿学习在不完美数据下的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人模仿学习方法常将演示中的失误和修正行为视为噪声，且现有的奖励/值模型多基于单调进度假设，忽略了执行过程中的局部错误和恢复机制，导致无法充分利用这些包含“纠错逻辑”的宝贵数据。

Method: 提出了 ReTVL（ReTry-Supervised Value Learning）框架，利用“重试（retry）”事件作为稀疏监督信号，通过结合全局进度标定与局部成对偏好学习，训练对错误敏感的值函数，并据此对演示片段进行重加权。

Result: 在真实机器人操控任务的实验中，ReTVL 展现了比基于进度的基准方法更细粒度的值估计能力，并显著提升了在非完美演示下的策略学习性能。

Conclusion: ReTVL 框架能够有效捕捉机器人演示中的局部错误与修正行为，通过重加权数据改进了下游的行为克隆效果，在处理包含失误的混合质量演示时，表现优于传统的基于进度假设的基准方法。

Abstract: Human demonstrations for robot imitation learning often contain mistakes and corrective behaviors, such as imprecise grasps, object misalignment, unstable contact, and repeated attempts. While these segments are commonly treated as noisy or suboptimal data, they provide valuable evidence about when execution deviates from a desirable path and how task feasibility can be restored. However, existing reward and value models often rely on monotonic progress assumptions, which capture coarse task advancement but may overlook local execution errors and corrective behaviors in imperfect demonstrations. In this work, we propose ReTVL (ReTry-Supervised Value Learning), a framework for learning mistake-sensitive value functions from mixed-quality robot demonstrations by leveraging retry events as sparse supervision. ReTVL captures the local degradation-and-recovery structure around mistakes by combining global progress calibration with local pairwise preference learning induced by sparsely annotated retry keypoints. The learned value model is then used to reweight demonstration chunks for downstream behavior cloning, reducing the influence of harmful execution errors while preserving useful corrective behaviors. Experiments on real-robot manipulation tasks show that ReTVL produces more fine-grained value estimates than progress-based baselines and improves imitation learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [176] [TACTFUL: Tactile-Driven Exploration For Object Localization and Identification in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24712)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Chung Hee Kim,Camilla Tabasso,Tye Brady,Joshua Migdal,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACTFUL是一种无需视觉的纯触觉自主探测框架，通过多指机器人在真实环境中的训练，实现了高效的未知物体探索、识别与几何重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够仅凭触觉识别物体，而现有机器人系统过度依赖视觉，在缺乏视觉输入的复杂环境下，难以实现自主触觉探索与物体识别。

Method: 提出“TACTFUL”框架，使用多指机器人进行无视觉触觉探索；完全在真实硬件上训练，而非模拟；采用单策略模型，通过动态奖励调度平衡全局空间探索与局部表面精细化。

Result: 在真实世界物体测试中取得77%的识别成功率，平均重构误差仅为0.015米，性能表现优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 触觉传感结合结构化学习，能够成为实现物体级推理的有效主要模态，证明了在视觉缺失的情况下，机器人依靠触觉进行自主探索和物体识别的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly locate and identify objects by touch alone, even without vision. In contrast, robotic systems rely heavily on vision and struggle with autonomous tactile exploration and object identification. We present TACTFUL, a vision-free tactile exploration framework that enables a multi-fingered robot to autonomously explore confined workspaces, discover objects through contact, and identify them via tactile reconstruction. Trained entirely on real hardware without simulation, our system learns a single policy that balances global workspace exploration with local surface refinement through a dynamic reward schedule. Our results demonstrate that tactile sensing, when paired with structured learning, can serve as an effective primary modality for object-level reasoning, achieving 77% success with 0.015 m average reconstruction error and outperforming baseline approaches on real-world objects.

</details>


### [177] [World Value Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24742)
*Zhihao Wang,Jianxiong Li,Yu Cui,Yuan Gao,Xianyuan Zhan,Junzhi Yu,Xiao Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有机器人价值模型时序建模不足的问题，本文提出了WVM（世界价值模型）。通过融合世界模型的时间预测能力，该模型实现了对混合质量数据的高效评估，并在专家及次优数据集上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于VLM的机器人价值模型因缺乏时序建模能力，难以准确评估动态任务；而世界模型天然擅长时序理解和未来规划，因此适合作为通用价值评估的基础。

Method: 提出WVM（世界价值模型），利用世界模型在时序建模和未来预测上的优势，将其整合至价值函数学习中，以实现对任务进程的准确评估。同时，引入了包含800条带有人工标注的次优轨迹数据集（Suboptimal-Value-Bench）用于评估模型的稳健性。

Result: WVM在标准基准测试中实现了SOTA级别的价值排序相关性（VOC）；在Suboptimal-Value-Bench中表现出良好的稳健性；且在模拟与真实环境的策略学习中，显著提升了机器人操作性能。

Conclusion: 世界模型（World Models）是构建通用价值评估模型的理想基石。提出的WVM通过结合世界模型的时间理解能力与价值估计，能够稳健地处理专家数据及非专家（次优）数据，从而有效提升大规模、混合质量机器人数据的学习效果。

Abstract: Generalist value models play a pivotal role in scaling robotic policy learning from large-scale, mixed-quality data. Mathematically, accurate value estimation demands deep temporal understanding, requiring models to both ground the current belief using historical context and plan over future outcomes. However, most existing robotic value models are built on Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbones that are pretrained primarily on static or temporally sparse visual observations, lacking the requisite temporal modeling capabilities for value estimation. Unlike VLMs, world models naturally excel at temporal modeling and future planning, making them ideal foundations for learning generalizable value functions. Driven by this insight, we marry world models with value estimation to construct a new generalist robotic value model, World Value Model (WVM), that offers accurate task progressions to assess data quality. On standard benchmarks, WVM delivers state-of-the-art (SOTA) Value-Order Correlation (VOC) results. Complementing standard evaluation suites that contains only expert data, we further introduce Suboptimal-Value-Bench, a multi-embodiment benchmark consisting of 800 suboptimal trajectories with high-fidelity, human-labeled frame annotations. Our evaluations show that WVM maintains its SOTA performance on Suboptimal-Value-Bench, establishing its robustness in handling both expert and suboptimal data. When deployed for policy learning, WVM improves manipulation performance across various policy extraction approaches in both simulated and real-world deployment, providing robust guidance for learning from mixed-quality data.

</details>


### [178] [Vision-Language Model Reasoning for Contextual Semantic Mapping in Intralogistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24814)
*Marvin Rüdt,Hao Pang,Constantin Enke,Zäzilia Seibold,Kai Furmans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将SLAM、SAM和VLM相结合的语义建图方法，实现了无需预定义类别的零样本物体语义与移动性感知，为机器人提供了更具上下文意识的地图表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物流环境中的自主移动机器人主要依赖几何地图，缺乏对物体语义类别及其上下文属性（如可移动性）的深入理解，难以应对动态变化的环境。

Method: 提出了一种结合SLAM几何建图、SAM实例分割、实例聚类以及VLM多视角推理的管线。该方法通过跨视角聚合观察数据，利用VLM在零样本、开放词表设置下推断物体的上下文属性（如可移动性），无需针对特定任务进行训练。

Result: 在语义分类任务上达到了98.93%的mIoU，在物体可移动性估计上达到了89.17%的mAcc，验证了方案的高准确性。
