Conclusion: 该流水线成功构建了包含几何、语义和动态特性的上下文语义地图，证明了利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行零样本上下文推理在室内物流环境中的有效性。实验指出VLM推理速度和实例聚类的准确性是当前的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots operating in intralogistics environments rely on geometric maps for localization and navigation, but lack semantic understanding of objects and their contextual properties. We present a contextual semantic mapping pipeline that combines SLAM-based geometric mapping, SAM-based instance segmentation, instance clustering, and VLM multi-view reasoning to produce a contextual semantic map representation encoding geometric structure, object class, and object movability. By aggregating observations across multiple viewpoints and querying a VLM in a zero-shot, open-vocabulary setting, the pipeline infers contextual object properties--here demonstrated through movability--without requiring task-specific training or predefined object categories. We evaluate three VLMs under two prompting strategies and conduct a component-wise analysis of the pipeline. The proposed pipeline achieves 98.93 % mIoU for semantic classification and 89.17 % mAcc for object movability estimation. Component analysis identifies VLM reasoning as the primary bottleneck for contextual understanding and instance clustering as the main limitation for panoptic performance. The resulting semantic map supports context-aware filtering and robust navigation in dynamic intralogistics environments.

</details>


### [179] [InSight: Self-Guided Skill Acquisition via Steerable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24884)
*Maggie Wang,Lars Osterberg,Stephen Tian,Ola Shorinwa,Jiajun Wu,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InSight是一个通过将VLA模型细化为“由语义原语控制”来实现自主技能获取的框架，它利用自动数据标注和VLM引导的数据飞轮，实现了无需人类干预的机器人技能持续学习与进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在操作技能上的表现受限于训练数据中所包含的技能范围，限制了其自主获取新技术的能力。

Method: 提出了InSight框架，包含两个核心阶段：1. 自动分割管线，利用VLM进行任务分解并结合末端执行器位姿将演示切分为带有语义标签的原始动作；2. VLM引导的数据飞轮（data flywheel），自动识别缺失任务的原始动作，通过VLM生成的底层控制进行自主演示采集、标注，并将成功的数据整合进VLA训练集。

Result: 在仿真和现实世界的多种任务（如翻转、关闭抽屉、扫地、扭转、倾倒）中验证了InSight的有效性。无需人类提供目标技能的演示，即可通过组合已学习的原始动作完成全新的、长周期的复杂任务。

Conclusion: 原始动作的可控性（primitive steerability）为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了实现持续技能获取的有效基础，使得模型能够无需额外人工演示即可完成新的长周期任务。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can learn manipulation skills from demonstrations, but their capabilities are bounded by the skills in the training data. We present InSight, a framework that unlocks autonomous skill acquisition by rendering VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level (e.g., "move gripper to the bowl", "lift upward", "pour the bottle"). InSight consists of two primary stages: (1) an automated segmentation pipeline that partitions demonstrations into labeled primitives via VLM plan decomposition and end-effector poses to enable VLA primitive steerability, and (2) a VLM-guided data flywheel that identifies missing primitives required to accomplish a novel task, autonomously attempts demonstrations of the missing primitives with VLM-proposed low-level control, and automatically labels, stores, and integrates successful demonstrations into the VLA training set. We evaluate InSight across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, including block flipping, drawer closing, sweeping, twisting, and pouring, without any human demonstrations of these target skills. Once learned, these primitives can be composed to execute novel, long-horizon tasks without additional human demonstrations. Our findings demonstrate that primitive steerability provides a practical foundation for continual skill acquisition in VLA policies. Project website: https://insight-vla.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 RIFT-Bench，这是一个基于图表示的自动化红队测试框架，能够为不同架构的代理式 AI 系统提供统一、可扩展的安全性评估和漏洞发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估方法局限于特定实现或领域，无法在异构的代理式 AI 系统间进行统一的比较，且代理式系统本身带来了复杂的新型攻击向量。

Method: 提出了一种基于图表示（Graph Representation-driven）的动态红队测试方法 RIFT-Bench，包含两个自动化阶段：1. 发现阶段（Discovery）：提取系统结构；2. 扫描阶段（Scanning）：部署自适应对抗攻击并生成评估报告。

Result: 在 45 个涵盖多种实现的代理式系统上验证了该方法的有效性，证明了其在异构代理架构中的泛化能力，并支持对缓解策略的直接评估。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench 提供了一个统一、可扩展且自动化程度高的框架，有效地解决了代理式 AI 系统在多样化架构下的安全评估挑战，为该领域的安全性评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report. It evaluates the examined system itself, leveraging a broad set of dynamically adaptable adversarial probes across diverse attack vectors and objectives. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation pipeline across 45 agentic systems spanning a diverse range of implementations, showing that the approach generalizes effectively to heterogeneous agentic architectures. Beyond systems and attacks, RIFT-Bench also supports direct evaluation of mitigation strategies. These key capabilities make RIFT-Bench a scalable foundation for security evaluation of agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [181] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao,Xiukun Huang,Boyu Lu,Junge Zhang,Mengjie Mao,Jiachen Li,Wei Xiong,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Neuro-Symbolic Drive通过将规则驱动规划器的内部符号决策逻辑转化为结构化链式推理，实现了VLA驾驶决策中推理与运动规划的深度耦合，显著提升了自动驾驶模型的准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于思维链（CoT）的VLA驾驶模型往往缺乏严格的步骤逻辑，导致推理过程与最终运动规划之间的因果关系薄弱。

Method: 提出一种“神经符号驾驶（Neuro-Symbolic Drive）”框架，通过从经典的规则驱动规划器（Rule-based planners）中提取内部执行轨迹（决策过程），将其转化为结构化的“规则接地推理（Rule-grounded reasoning）”，并以此监督微调Qwen3.5-4B VLA模型。

Result: 在模拟基准测试中，该方法显著提升了驾驶性能。在三相机视角下，ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26，漏检率（Miss Rate）从8.30%降至6.40%；在八相机视角下，ADE@3s从0.54降至0.26，漏检率从10.13%降至5.99%。

Conclusion: 通过神经符号规划逻辑，该框架有效解决了传统VLA模型中推理与决策因果脱节的问题，为驾驶任务提供了更具可解释性和准确性的推理范式。

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: https://github.com/XiangboGaoBarry/Neural-Symbolic-Drive.

</details>


### [182] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文旨在明确“AI代理”的定义，提出了区分外挂式工作流（agentic）与内生自主系统（agentive）的五个核心维度，并据此提出了GIC架构，旨在实现具有真实自主性、可审计且受控的通用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前大模型领域中“智能体（Agent）”概念泛化的问题，旨在厘清自动化系统与具备真实代理权（agency）系统之间的界限，以提升系统开发能力并缓解对“机器代理权”带来的安全顾虑。

Method: 1. 理论分析：结合笛卡尔的独立思想理论与科幻作品中的自主体概念，确立评估AI代理的五个维度（目标、身份、决策、自我调节、学习）。
2. 模型构建：提出“目标-身份-配置器”（Goal-Identity-Configurator, GIC）架构，整合层级目标分解、身份演化、世界模型下的模拟推理及自我指导学习。

Result: 通过对五个维度的架构分析，明确了系统能力内化（internalization）是区分真代理与伪代理的关键，并构建了一个具备通用自主能力的GIC模型框架。

Conclusion: 文章区分了“agentic”（基于外部工程工作流的自动化系统）和“agentive”（具有内生自主能力的系统），并建议真正的代理权（agency）应源于系统内部结构的整合，而非外部脚手架。在此基础上提出了GIC架构，并为高自主系统下的可审计性与安全性提供了思路。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [183] [Accelerating Disaggregated RL for Visual Generative LLMs with Diffusion-Based Parallelism and Trainer-Assisted Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24369)
*Sijie Wang,Zhengyu Qing,Zhiqiang Tan,Yiming Yin,Yeqing Zhang,Yaoyuan Wang,Qiang Wang,Xiaowen Chu,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DigenRL是一个面向扩散模型强化学习的解耦框架，通过细粒度流水线和弹性算力调度策略，解决了资源耦合瓶颈，实现了显著的训练吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型强化学习（RL）框架（如veRL-Omni）多采用同构部署，存在资源耦合、扩缩容受限及流水线气泡严重等问题，难以支持大规模扩散生成模型的高效训练。

Method: 1. 提出解耦RL架构，实现训练与推理资源的独立扩展与异构部署；2. 引入生成轴流水线 (GAP) 和时间步并行 (TSP) 以细化任务调度；3. 采用弹性训练器辅助生成 (TAG) 动态调配算力；4. 实现一种紧凑的单步约束异步策略，最小化流水线气泡。

Result: 在16-32块GPU的多个平台及多个主流模型（HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B等）实验中，DigenRL的吞吐量较现有最先进系统（veRL-Omni, GenRL）提升了1.56-2.10倍。

Conclusion: DigenRL通过创新的解耦架构、流水线优化及弹性计算方案，成功解决了扩散模型RL训练中存在的资源耦合与效率低下问题，显著提升了训练吞吐量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, driving the emergence of high-performance RL systems such as veRL for autoregressive large language models (LLMs). In parallel, diffusion-oriented RL algorithms, e.g., DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, have rapidly expanded the scope of RL from language reasoning to diffusion-based visual and flow-based generation. However, efficient RL systems for diffusion generative LLMs remain underexplored. Existing implementations, e.g., veRL-Omni, still rely on colocated execution, which simplifies synchronization but couples rollout and training resources, limits heterogeneous deployment, and constrains independent scaling.
  To this end, we introduce DigenRL, a disaggregated RL framework for diffusion-based generative LLMs that supports flexible resource allocation, accommodates heterogeneous GPUs, and facilitates efficient task scheduling. To maximally reduce the execution bubbles in the disaggregated architecture, we propose: 1) a generation-axis pipeline (GAP) and time-step parallelism (TSP) in the diffusion architecture to enable finer-grained pipelining between rollout and training; 2) an elastic trainer-assisted generation (TAG) approach to enable the trainer GPU resources to dynamically assist in executing rollout generations; and 3) a tightly one-step constrained asynchronous strategy to further utilize the tail bubble in the pipeline. Extensive experiments are conducted on three hardware testbeds with 16-32 GPUs using HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B, FLUX.1-12B, and QwenImage-20B generative models. Experimental results show that DigenRL achieves 1.56-2.10x throughput improvements over state-of-the-art diffusion RL systems, veRL-Omni and GenRL.

</details>


### [184] [Can Aggregate Invariants Accelerate Continuous Subgraph Matching? Limits, Laws, and a Dynamic Spectral Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24421)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了谱过滤在连续子图匹配（CSM）中的应用。研究发现，虽然谱过滤能显著减少候选集规模，但其对基于邻接的递归探索过程无实质性加速。文章通过选择性维护局部谱索引，成功实现了高效的算法更新，并在实验中揭示了该技术对构建和扫描阶段的显著优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱过滤技术在静态子图匹配中表现优异（如拉普拉斯交错定理），但其在动态图场景下，维护计算成本与过滤效果之间的平衡尚不明确。该研究旨在探讨谱过滤能否有效加速连续子图匹配（CSM），并解决动态更新下的性能衰减问题。

Method: 1. 理论量化：形式化扰动松弛，证明懒惰维护的谱界在动态更新下的失效速度。 2. 算法设计：提出了一种选择性的精确局部谱维护策略，通过仅在必要时计算小邻域谱，实现微秒级的更新。 3. 实验验证：在解耦的CSM基准测试中，通过消融实验比较了是否使用谱过滤的性能差异。 4. 方法论：提出了“中间体不变性”评估方法，利用半径分层工作负载验证过滤器的有效性和边界条件。

Result: 1. 理论上证明谱过滤在动态场景下极易失效。 2. 实验表明，该方法可剔除高达51%的候选者或跳过47%的更新枚举。 3. 在特定构造的辐射分层工作负载中，该技术实现了99.9%的中间体减少和748倍的速度提升。 4. 然而，在常规查询中，它对枚举中间体数量几乎无影响，仅加速了构造和扫描步骤。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，谱过滤对于连续子图匹配（CSM）的加速效果主要集中在候选集构建和列表扫描阶段，但对基于邻接的探索（即枚举过程）几乎无加速效果。研究提出了一种中间体不变性方法论，并证明了通过选择性维护局部谱能有效平衡计算开销与过滤效益。

Abstract: Spectral filtering recently delivered substantial pruning for \emph{static} subgraph matching: Laplacian interlacing rejects candidates whose neighborhoods cannot host the query. We study whether such aggregate structural tests can accelerate \emph{continuous} subgraph matching (CSM) over dynamic graphs, and answer in three parts. First, lazily maintained spectral bounds are infeasible exactly where spectral pruning has value: we characterize the tightest safe rule over a formalized perturbation relaxation and show that even it loses essentially all pruning power within four touching updates. Second, exact maintenance is affordable when selective: pruning utility and recomputation cost are anti-correlated across vertices -- hubs provably never prune -- so recomputing small-neighborhood spectra on touch sustains exact local spectra at microseconds per update, complete by construction. Third, integrated into a decoupled CSM benchmark against an identical-minus-spectra control, the tests remove up to $51\%$ of candidates or safely skip up to $47\%$ of update enumerations, yet enumeration intermediates remain unchanged -- beyond the gates' skipped first-level bindings, typically zero -- across two engines, four real graphs, two stream types, and $77$ solved queries; a constructed radius-stratified workload confirms the instrument detects the exception when one exists ($-99.9\%$ intermediates, $748\times$ faster). Aggregate tests accelerate what scales with candidate sets -- construction, list scans -- never adjacency-guided exploration. We distill an intermediate-invariance methodology for evaluating CSM filters and release a reusable dynamic local-spectra index.

</details>


### [185] [Safe and Generalizable Hierarchical Multi-Agent RL via Constraint Manifold Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24010)
*Zihao Guo,Jianing Zhao,Ling Li,Hao Liang,Giuseppe Loianno,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，通过底层约束流形确保理论上的硬安全，同时利用高层策略学习实现高效协调，解决了学习方法性能与安全性的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在学习方法的性能与控制理论方法的安全性之间存在权衡，现有基于学习的方法缺乏安全保障，而基于控制理论的方法往往过于保守且效率低下。

Method: 采用分层多智能体强化学习框架：底层利用“约束流形”（constraint manifold）实施硬安全约束，高层负责策略学习以实现多智能体协调；通过该机制确保训练过程的平稳动力学。

Result: 在实验中实现了具有竞争力的性能，保持了近乎完美的安全率，并能有效泛化至不同数量的智能体与障碍物场景。

Conclusion: 提出一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，通过约束流形在保障理论安全性的同时实现有效的多智能体协调，在复杂环境任务中实现了高性能、高安全率与良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are widely used in safety-critical applications that require coordinated behavior under strict safety constraints. Existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: learning-based methods achieve strong empirical performance but lack theoretical safety guarantees, while control-theoretic methods enforce safety but often lead to overly conservative and inefficient behaviors. We propose a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enforces hard safety constraints under mild assumptions at low level via a constraint manifold, while enabling effective coordination through high-level policy learning. Our approach provides theoretical safety guarantees in the multi-agent setting and yields stationary learning dynamics, thereby enabling stable and efficient training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive performance while maintaining nearly perfect safety rates, and generalizes effectively to varying numbers of agents and obstacles.

</details>


### [186] [Reinforcement Learning Towards Broadly and Persistently Beneficial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24014)
*Akshay V. Jagadeesh,Rahul K. Arora,Khaled Saab,Ali Malik,Mikhail Trofimov,Foivos Tsimpourlas,Johannes Heidecke,Karan Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，在真实场景中通过强化学习（RL）训练“有益特质”，可以显著提升AI模型在未见任务中的对齐泛化能力，并增强其应对恶意干扰的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署在更多样化和高风险的领域，需要确保模型在训练分布之外也能保持对齐。当前的强化学习训练容易产生奖励破解、欺骗等非预期行为，因此迫切需要研究如何通过强化有益行为，实现模型对齐能力的广泛且持久的泛化。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含健康、科学、教育等领域中衡量“有益特质”（如诚实、公平、风险意识、可纠正性）的真实数据集；2. 对模型进行针对这些有益行为的强化学习（RL）训练；3. 在50多个独立的对齐和有益行为基准测试上进行评估，并对比计算资源匹配的基线模型。

Result: 1. 与基线模型相比，该方法在超过80%的分布外（OOD）基准测试中表现更优；2. 观察到了明显的对齐迁移现象（如仅在健康领域训练，也能提升非健康领域的对齐表现）；3. 模型表现出更强的对齐持久性，对对抗性提示和有害微调的抵抗力更强。

Conclusion: 通过在真实场景中对有益行为进行强化学习（RL），能够培养出具备广泛且持久泛化能力的对齐模型。这种方法是提升AI系统在复杂、高风险环境中与人类价值观保持一致的有效途径。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse and high-stakes settings, model alignment must generalize beyond the tasks and domains seen during training. This is especially important for reinforcement learning (RL), which can introduce unexpected misalignment through reward hacking, deception, or other unintended strategies. We study whether RL on beneficial behavior, instantiated in realistic domains, can produce broad and persistent alignment generalization beyond the training distribution. We construct a dataset of realistic situations designed to measure and train beneficial traits, such as truthfulness, fairness, risk awareness, and corrigibility, spanning varied domains, including health, science, and education. We then train models with RL on this dataset and evaluate them on more than 50 independent benchmarks of alignment and beneficial behavior. Compared to a compute-matched baseline, beneficial trait RL improves performance on over 80% of these out-of-distribution benchmarks. We observe substantial out-of-distribution alignment transfer: a beneficial-behavior RL intervention entirely limited to one domain, health, produces broad improvements on non-health alignment evaluations, including reduced reward hacking, deception, and general misalignment. Finally, we study alignment persistence: whether behavior remains robustly aligned under attempts to steer models towards misalignment. Models trained with beneficial trait RL show improved persistence, including greater resistance to adversarial prompting and harmful finetuning; further work is required to isolate the sources of these effects. These results suggest that RL to reinforce beneficial behavior in realistic domains can produce models that are more robustly aligned with human flourishing.

</details>


### [187] [AI Tokenomics: The Economics of Tokens, Computation, and Pricing in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24616)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“AI代币经济学”概念框架，将代币作为量化AI系统技术成本与经济价值的核心单位，旨在优化资源配置并指导AI市场设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代币已成为现代基础模型服务的核心会计单位，涵盖了计算、存储、能源与经济价值，亟需一个系统化的理论框架来分析其生成、消费、定价与优化机制。

Method: 构建了连接技术成本、生产函数、资源分配、测量方法与市场设计的综合分析框架，将代币作为现代基础模型服务的核心会计单位进行建模。

Result: 明确了代币支出与经济价值之间的差异，指出代币的经济价值受制于复杂的生产环境和传播效应，而非简单的成本加成。

Conclusion: 通过建立AI代币经济学框架，揭示了经济价值由边际生产率、工作流位置、推理活动及风险等多种因素决定；并提出了针对隐性代币测量、经验校准、代币生产率及市场设计等未来研究方向。

Abstract: Tokens have become the practical accounting unit for modern foundation model services, linking information processing, computation, memory use, energy expenditure, pricing, and economic value. This paper develops a framework for AI tokenomics: the study of how tokens are generated, consumed, priced, allocated, and optimized across AI systems. We connect token-level technical costs to workflow-level production functions, enterprise resource allocation, measurement and instrumentation methods, and emerging market-design questions. The framework shows that token expenditure and economic value are distinct: value depends on marginal productivity, workflow position, hidden reasoning activity, risk, and downstream propagation effects. The paper concludes by identifying open research directions in hidden-token measurement, empirical calibration, token productivity, dynamic allocation, and token-based markets.

</details>


### [188] [Can Language Model Agents be Helpful Circuit Explainers in Mechanistic Interpretability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24026)
*Ayan Antik Khan,Harsh Kohli,Yuekun Yao,Huan Sun,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了名为 HyVE 的代理框架，旨在利用语言模型自动解释已定位的神经电路。通过在 AgenticInterpBench 上的实验验证，该方法展现了有效性，但也揭示了因果验证环节仍是制约解释可靠性的最大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机械可解释性领域虽然在自动定位电路方面取得进展，但在解释这些组件的功能时仍高度依赖人工，缺乏标准化，亟需自动化且可靠的解释方法。

Method: 引入了 AgenticInterpBench 基准测试；提出了 HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain) 代理框架，通过观察、假设生成和因果验证的迭代循环来解释模型组件。

Result: HyVE 在四种不同语言模型骨干网络上均能生成有用的组件级和任务级解释；分析发现强模型更擅长生成基于证据的假设，但失败往往发生在验证环节（如验证计划不完整、代码执行错误）。在 Llama-3-8B 上的案例研究证明了该方法可扩展至真实训练的模型。

Conclusion: LM 代理在解释模型电路方面表现出潜力，但验证阶段（特别是因果验证的可靠性）仍是当前的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has made substantial progress in automatically localizing circuits, but explaining what localized components do remains labor-intensive and difficult to standardize. In this work, we study whether language model (LM) agents can assist with this explanation problem once a circuit has already been identified. We introduce AgenticInterpBench, a benchmark for circuit explanation built from 84 semi-synthetic transformer circuits with 163 component-level annotations. We propose HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain), an agentic explainer that analyzes each component through an iterative loop of observation, hypothesis generation, and causal validation, eventually producing a component-level explanation and a circuit-level task description. Across four LM backbones, HyVE recovers useful component- and task-level explanations, but no backbone is uniformly best. Our analysis shows that strong backbones usually form observation-grounded hypotheses, while failures more often arise later in the validation loop, through incomplete validation plans, code execution errors, or unresolved hypotheses. A case study on an arithmetic circuit in Llama-3-8B shows that the same formulation can extend beyond semi-synthetic benchmarks to naturally trained models. Overall, LM agents are promising circuit explainers, but reliable validation remains the key obstacle.

</details>


### [189] [Abstractions of Queries in Ontology-Based Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24618)
*Michel Leclère,Marie-Laure Mugnier,Guillaume Pérution-Kihli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了OBDA中的查询抽象问题，通过扩展UCQ查询语言，实现了对最小完备抽象的表示，并利用数据交换理论刻画了最大可靠抽象，在不增加计算复杂度的情况下优化了抽象效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在规则支持的OBDA中，由于完美查询抽象并不总是存在，为解决如何有效进行查询抽象（query abstraction），研究最小完备（minimally complete）和最大可靠（maximally sound）抽象的表达能力与性质。

Method: 在基于存在规则和确定答案语义的OBDA框架下，研究了带受限不等式和特定常数标记谓词的UCQ扩展，并结合数据交换（data exchange）领域的最大恢复（maximum recovery）理论来刻画最大可靠抽象。

Result: 该研究证明了所提的UCQ扩展并未增加查询评价问题的计算复杂度，同时提升了抽象的表达能力，使其能够处理完美抽象，并为最大可靠抽象提供了理论特征刻画。

Conclusion: 通过扩展UCQ（引入受限不等式和标记数据库常量的特殊谓词），研究者成功表达了最小完备抽象（在存在完美抽象时即为完美抽象），并利用数据交换中的“最大恢复”概念刻画了最大可靠抽象。

Abstract: In ontology-based data access (OBDA), multiple data sources are integrated via mappings to an ontology. We consider an OBDA setting based on existential rules and the certain answer semantics. We address the recent issue of query abstraction, which consists of abstracting data queries by translating them to the ontology layer. Since a perfect abstraction may not exist, the notions of minimally complete and maximally sound abstractions have been introduced.
  We study abstractions within an extension of UCQs with a limited form of inequality and a special predicate marking database constants. While this extension does not lead to an increased complexity of the problems of interest, it is able to express minimally complete abstractions, hence perfect abstractions when they exist. We also characterize maximally sound abstractions by making a new connection with the notion of maximum recovery stemming from data exchange.

</details>


### [190] [Breaking the Filter Bubble: A Semantic Pareto-DQN Framework for Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24042)
*Cláudio Lúcio Do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva,André de Oliveira Brandão*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种Pareto-DQN多目标强化学习框架，通过将参与度、多样性和公平性视为独立奖励，有效解决了推荐系统“信息茧房”问题，实现了用户参与度与社会价值的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推荐系统过度关注即时用户参与度，导致了严重的“信息茧房”和“语义同质化”问题；传统单目标模型（如Deep Q-Network）无法平衡平台留存率与多样性、公平性等社会价值之间的权衡。

Method: 提出了一种语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程（Semantic Multi-Objective MDP）框架，并引入Pareto-DQN智能体。该方法将用户参与度、信息多样性和提供商公平性视为独立的非聚合奖励信号，利用超体积（hypervolume）最大化策略进行动作选择，避免了静态奖励标量化带来的局限。

Result: 在MovieLens数据集上的实验表明，Pareto-DQN能有效打破导致语义崩溃的反馈循环，维持较高的状态轨迹差异性，在提升多样性和公平性的前提下，仅对用户参与度产生微小影响。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Pareto-DQN的强化学习框架，能够有效缓解推荐系统中的信息茧房和语义同质化问题，为构建负责任且具备价值对齐的推荐系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recommender systems often induce filter bubbles and semantic homogenization by monolithically optimizing for immediate user engagement. Standard single-objective models, including traditional Deep Q-Networks, are ill-equipped to navigate the trade-offs between platform retention and critical societal values like information diversity and provider fairness. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework that formalizes recommendation as a semantic multi-objective Markov decision process. By integrating high-fidelity semantic embeddings with a Pareto-DQN agent, our architecture treats engagement, diversity, and fairness as distinct, non-aggregable reward signals, avoiding the pitfalls of static reward scalarization. Empirical evaluations on the MovieLens small dataset shows that our hypervolume based action selection disrupts the feedback loops responsible for semantic collapse. By sustaining high state-trajectory variance, the Pareto-DQN effectively maps the Pareto frontier, achieving gains in auxiliary societal objectives with only marginal impacts on engagement. This work provides a path toward intrinsically aligned, responsible recommender systems.

</details>


### [191] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对女性性工作者心理健康风险，本文提出一种基于集成特征选择与哈里斯鹰算法调优的逻辑回归模型，通过可解释AI识别关键致病因素，在实证中展现了高预测精度，为弱势群体的精准心理健康干预提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型难以捕获女性性工作者（FSW）中存在的复杂高维心理健康风险模式。针对该群体面临的暴力、社会污名及经济困难等导致的抑郁风险，需开发更精准的预测工具以辅助早期诊断与干预。

Method: 提出了一种混合预测模型：1. 特征选择：结合ANOVA与互信息（Mutual Information）的集成策略；2. 分类器：使用哈里斯鹰优化算法（Harris Hawks Optimization）调优的逻辑回归模型；3. 可解释性：通过可解释AI（XAI）方法解析风险特征权重。

Result: 模型在包含3,005名受试者的样本中表现优异，准确率达95.78%，F1分数达95.77%，AUC值为0.96。研究识别出创伤后应激障碍、客户相关暴力及职业因素是导致抑郁的主要贡献因子。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了一种高性能、可解释的混合机器学习模型，证明了集成学习与群体智能优化在预测性工作者心理健康方面的有效性。该模型通过揭示创伤及职业风险因素，为弱势群体的精准心理卫生干预政策及早期心理评价工具的开发提供了科学依据。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Trajectory Imitation: Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24064)
*Tianyuan Shi,Canbin Huang,Bei Li,Xin Chen,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型蒸馏中小模型只会模仿轨迹的问题，提出了SGPO框架，通过蒸馏结构化策略代替轨迹模仿，显著提升了推理能力与泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理蒸馏方法（如SFT）主要通过模仿具体解题轨迹，导致小模型倾向于“死记硬背”实例而非掌握通用推理逻辑，限制了其在未知问题上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了“策略引导策略优化”（SGPO）框架：1. 提取结构化策略描述；2. 构建自主与策略引导的轨迹对比；3. 采用基于Token级的Forward-KL目标进行分布蒸馏；4. 引入自适应实例加权机制，根据模型能力动态调节引导强度。

Result: 在四个数学基准测试中，SGPO超越了SFT、在线强化学习及混合策略基线，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上将平均分提升了2.2个点。

Conclusion: SGPO通过从“轨迹模仿”转向“策略蒸馏”，有效提升了小模型在复杂推理任务上的泛化能力，证明了显式策略指导在知识蒸馏中的优越性。

Abstract: Distilling reasoning capabilities from strong to weak language models typically involves imitating specific solution trajectories, effectively transferring what to answer rather than how to reason. This trajectory-level imitation encourages memorization of instance-specific steps rather than acquisition of transferable problem-solving skills, limiting generalization to novel problems. We propose Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization (SGPO), which replaces instance-level trajectory imitation with reusable strategy distillation. SGPO extracts structured strategy descriptions from strong-model responses and, for each problem, constructs both autonomous and strategy-guided trajectories to enable direct comparison of the model's behavior with and without strategic guidance. The framework then addresses two key questions. For how to distill, a token-level forward-KL objective selectively transfers the distributional shift induced by strategy conditioning into the unguided policy, with proximal constraints ensuring stability. For when to distill, adaptive instance-level weighting strengthens guidance when autonomous exploration falls short and reduces it as the model's own competence grows. Experiments on four mathematical benchmarks across two model families show that SGPO consistently outperforms SFT, on-policy RL, and hybrid-policy baselines, improving the average score by 2.2 points over the strongest baseline on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Analysis reveals that the forward-KL objective provides an inherently selective distillation signal that outperforms direct trajectory imitation, and that strategy distillation exhibits complementary scaling with base model capability.

</details>


### [193] [Exploring Academic Influence of Algorithms by Co-occurrence Network Based on Full-text of Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24099)
*Yuzhuo Wang,Chengzhi Zhang,Min Song,Seong Deok Kim,Youngsoo Ko,Juhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在大规模文献语料中构建算法共现网络，量化揭示了算法如何在时间维度上形成复杂的群体影响力结构，并发现算法在网络中的核心地位对其影响力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多孤立地衡量单个算法的影响力，忽视了学术论文中算法之间的互联性及其在复杂网络中形成的集体影响力。因此，本研究旨在通过网络视角深入剖析人工智能时代科学研究中算法的演化与影响力。

Method: 基于自然语言处理（NLP）技术，利用深度学习模型从学术论文全文中提取算法实体，构建了涵盖过去四十年的整体、累积及年度算法共现网络；应用多种中心性度量指标评估算法的群体影响力及其演化趋势。

Result: 算法网络表现出明显的复杂网络特征，连接密度在过去二十年间显著增加；高影响力的算法通常占据网络核心位置，具有更强的控制力与中心性；算法影响力的衰退过程遵循“先失去核心位置，再削弱关联性”的规律。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建学术论文算法共现网络，揭示了算法影响力不仅取决于个体指标，更取决于其在网络中的结构地位。研究证实，算法的统治地位与共现网络的结构特征密切相关，并为未来探索算法、学者与研究任务之间的复杂关联提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Algorithms have become central to scientific research in the era of artificial intelligence (AI). Although algorithm mentions in papers are often used to indicate popularity and influence, existing studies usually evaluate individual algorithms in isolation and pay limited attention to the collective influence formed through their interconnections. This study constructs large-scale algorithm co-occurrence networks in natural language processing (NLP) based on the full text of academic papers and investigates algorithm influence from a network perspective. Using deep learning models, we extract algorithm entities and build overall, cumulative, and annual co-occurrence networks. We analyze their structural characteristics and apply multiple centrality measures to assess the group influence of algorithms across the whole field and over time. The results show that algorithm networks display typical features of complex networks, with increasingly dense connections developing over approximately two decades. Classic, high-performing algorithms and those located at the intersections of different research periods tend to have high popularity, control, centrality, and balanced influence. When the influence of an algorithm declines, it usually loses its core network position first, followed by weaker associations with other algorithms. This study is the first large-scale analysis of algorithm co-occurrence networks. Covering more than four decades of academic publications, it provides a temporal and structural view of algorithm influence and offers a foundation for future research on networks linking algorithms, scholars, and tasks.

</details>


### [194] [ReMMD: Realistic Multilingual Multi-Image Agentic Verification for Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24112)
*Chenhao Dang,Dantong Zhu,Jun Yang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现实中复杂的多语言、多图像虚假信息检测难题，本文提出了 ReMMD 框架，包含涵盖多元且真实数据的基准测试集 ReMMDBench 以及核心检测智能体 ReMMD-Agent，实现了高准确率且低成本的证据验证与虚假信息检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测方法多局限于短文本、单图像或二分类，难以应对现实中包含多语言叙事、多图像、来源复杂及多模态暗示的真实虚假信息，且现有的代理验证（agentic verification）成本过高。

Method: 提出了 ReMMDBench 基准测试集，并开发了 ReMMD-Agent。该智能体采用“原子化分解-构建可复用证据集-输出结构化预测”的方法，通过持久记忆机制对长文本和多图像进行协同分析，以实现五分类真实性判断及八种畸变类型的检测。

Result: ReMMD-Agent 在五分类真实性任务中表现最佳，准确率达 41.80%，宏观 F1 分数达 39.12%；与现有主流代理系统相比，成本分别降低了 17.5% 和 79.9%。

Conclusion: ReMMD 框架通过其创新的 Agentic 验证机制，在处理复杂、多模态、多语言的虚假信息检测任务上表现出卓越的性能和成本效益，为现实世界的多模态虚假信息检测提供了更稳健的评估标准和解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection is increasingly important because viral posts now combine long multilingual narratives, several images, mixed provenance, and subtle text--image framing errors. Existing benchmarks and methods remain poorly matched to this setting: they usually isolate short captions, single images, binary labels, or one manipulation source, while agentic verification remains costly under realistic evidence search. We present ReMMD, a realistic multilingual multi-image agentic verification framework for multimodal misinformation detection. ReMMD includes ReMMDBench, a real-world multimodal misinformation detection benchmark with 500 samples, 2,756 images, five monolingual languages, two cross-lingual settings, three text-length tiers, multi-image posts, five-way veracity labels, eight distortion labels, evidence provenance, and rationales. It also includes ReMMD-Agent, a persistent-memory verifier that decomposes posts into atomic points, builds a reusable evidence set, and predicts structured L1/L2/L3 outputs. Across proprietary systems, open LVLMs, MMD-Agent, and T2-Agent, ReMMD-Agent obtains the best five-way veracity performance, with 41.80% accuracy and 39.12% macro-F1 using GPT-5.2, while reducing cost by 17.5% relative to MMD-Agent and 79.9% relative to T2-Agent. The project is available at https://dang-ai.github.io/ReMMD.

</details>


### [195] [VeryTrace: Verifying Reasoning Traces through Compilable Formalism and Structured Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24124)
*Ninghan Zhong,Ahmet Ege Tanriverdi,Kaan Kale,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对CoT推理中的错误传播与幻觉问题，本文提出VeryTrace框架，通过将自然语言思维链结构化为DSL并结合混合验证机制，实现了零样本下的步骤级精确诊断与错误修复，显著增强了LLM推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步思维链(CoT)推理具有脆弱性，早期的逻辑错误或幻觉会向后传播，导致后续推论虽表面顺畅但结论错误，且缺乏有效的纠错机制。

Method: 提出一种零样本验证与修复框架VeryTrace，包括：1. 定义特定领域语言(DSL)将推理步骤转化为结构化、可编译表示（明确依赖关系、量化表达式及演绎模式）；2. 采用混合验证策略，结合确定性计算检查与针对语义判断的LLM审计，实现步骤级错误定位与修复。

Result: 在AIME 2025数学竞赛、LLM-BabyBench机器人规划以及CLUTRR亲属关系推理三个领域中，在无需特定领域训练或少样本示例的情况下，相比零样本基线模型，准确率得到显著提升。

Conclusion: VeryTrace通过将自然语言转化为结构化表示与混合验证机制，证明了形式化追踪验证能够有效提升大模型在复杂任务中的推理精度与泛化能力，避免了传统CoT推理中早期错误传播的问题。

Abstract: Multi-step reasoning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting remains fragile: logical errors or hallucinations in early steps silently propagate, producing confident but incorrect conclusions. This paper presents VeryTrace, a zero-shot verification-and-repair framework that formalizes natural-language reasoning traces into a structured, compilable representation. VeryTrace introduces a Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that (i) makes step dependencies explicit, (ii) mechanizes quantitative content as executable expressions, and (iii) structures semantic inferences via deduction schemas. Our hybrid verifier combines deterministic checks for computational correctness, dependency resolution, and constraint satisfaction with targeted LLM audits for non-mechanizable semantic judgments, enabling step-level error localization and repair.
  Across three diverse domains-competition mathematics (AIME 2025), robotics planning (LLM-BabyBench), and kinship reasoning (CLUTRR), VeryTrace improves accuracy over zero-shot baselines on state-of-the-art LLMs without requiring domain-specific training or in-context examples, demonstrating that formalized trace verification achieves both precision and generalization.

</details>


### [196] [OmniPath: A Multi-Modal Agentic Framework for Auditing Wheelchair Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24129)
*ASM Mobarak Hossain,Nadim Mahmud,Vaskar Raychoudhury,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniPath 系统结合航空 LiDAR 和网络拓扑数据，通过自动化虚拟遍历技术定量分析路面坡度与障碍，为轮椅使用者提供比传统地图更精准的无障碍导航指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有地图服务（如 OSM）仅标示路径位置但缺乏物理通行质量信息（如路面坡度、障碍物）的问题，从而消除轮椅使用者面临的“隐形”出行障碍。

Method: 构建了 OmniPath 系统，融合 OpenStreetMap (OSM) 网络拓扑与 USGS 3DEP 高密度航空 LiDAR 数据，通过构建 3D 环境模型进行虚拟遍历；以 0.5 米为单位，根据 ADA 标准量化坡度（纵向/横向）与垂直不连续性，并计算加权严重性评分。

Result: 通过 200 次实地调研验证，该系统对高危障碍的识别具有较强的诊断可靠性，“严重”和“致命”类别的 F1 分数分别达到 0.60 和 0.58。

Conclusion: OmniPath 利用高密度 LiDAR 数据实现了自动化的微观环境评估，能有效识别传统地图无法覆盖的无障碍通行障碍，为轮椅使用者提供比地理位置更具实际导航价值的“可达性”信息。

Abstract: For a wheelchair user, a standard blue line on a map is often a broken promise. While platforms like OpenStreetMap (OSM) successfully capture where a path is, they frequently fail to convey how it physically feels to travel on it. This information barrier is problematic for wheelchair users. To solve this issue, we present OmniPath, a system that moves from passive mapping to proactive environmental auditing. Our framework fuses the network topology of OSM with the submeter precision of high-density aerial LiDAR (USGS 3DEP) to create a high-fidelity 3D model of the pedestrian environment. Rather than simply routing a user, our agent virtually traverses the network, analyzing the surface in 0.5 meter increments. It rigorously quantifies physical friction points specifically running slope, cross slope, and vertical discontinuities against ADA compliance standards, calculating a weighted severity score to categorize hazards from ``Mild'' to ``Critical.'' To ensure real world reliability, we validated the system against 200 physical ground truth field surveys across the National Mall using stratified random sampling. The framework demonstrated strong diagnostic reliability for high-severity hazards, achieving F1-scores of 0.60 for Severe and 0.58 for critical categories. By automating this micro-scale inspection, OmniPath identifies the ``invisible'' barriers that standard maps miss, effectively transforming a static dataset into accessibility data source that anticipates accessibility challenges before the user ever leaves home.

</details>


### [197] [T2D-Bench: Evidence-Gated Evaluation of LLM Outputs for Type 2 Diabetes Using a Multi-Layer Clinical-Lifestyle Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24145)
*Saba A. Farahani,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 T2D-Bench 框架，通过引入知识图谱驱动的证据门控机制，量化并修正了大模型在 2 型糖尿病临床建议中缺乏循证支持的遗漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型（LLM）在生成 2 型糖尿病临床建议时虽然语言流畅，但常忽视临床指南约束，且对生活方式与血糖影响的关联缺乏明确证据支持。

Method: 构建了 T2D-Bench，包含一个多层临床-生活方式知识图谱（结合 UMLS、DrugBank、SIDER 及 ADA 诊疗指南）；提出了证据门控（evidence-gated）评估框架，利用图可检查的证据路径对模型输出进行审计，并使用约束性修改（constrained revision）来修正输出。

Result: 在包含诊断、用药安全及生活方式冲突的 100 个结构化案例中，GPT-4o-mini 和 GPT-4o 分别有 35% 和 33% 的输出未能通过证据路径检查；应用证据门控后，模型能够实现更高水平的合规性。

Conclusion: 通过引入可计算的证据约束，可以有效识别、量化并纠正大模型在糖尿病临床建议中存在的非支持性遗漏问题，提升了输出结果的合规性与可信度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce clinically fluent recommendations for type 2 diabetes while failing to satisfy guideline constraints or explicitly justify lifestyle-related glycemic claims. We present T2D-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evidence-gated evaluation framework for testing whether LLM outputs satisfy explicit, graph-checkable evidence requirements. T2D-Bench is built on a multi-layer clinical-lifestyle knowledge graph that combines a biomedical spine (UMLS, DrugBank, SIDER), computable ADA Standards of Care rules, and lifestyle knowledge connected through a mechanistic bridge to glycemic laboratory effects. Across 100 structured vignettes spanning diagnosis, medication safety, and adversarial lifestyle conflicts, baseline outputs failed benchmark-defined evidence-path checks in 35% of cases for GPT-4o-mini and 33% for GPT-4o. The evidence gate detects unsupported omissions and uses constrained revision to bring outputs into verifier-level compliance with benchmark-defined evidence requirements. These results show that computable evidence constraints can make unsupported clinical omissions explicit, measurable, and correctable in diabetes-focused LLM outputs.

</details>


### [198] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文通过 Wasserstein 黎曼流形理论统一了扩散模型与流匹配，指出扩散模型通过梯度流演化，而流匹配遵循最短路径（测地线），两者构成了生成模型在最优传输几何下的完整图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型（DDPM, DDIM, NCSN 等）与流匹配模型常被视为独立的理论框架，作者旨在通过 Wasserstein 几何揭示二者在数学上的深层联系，从而提供一个统一的生成模型分析体系。

Method: 利用 Wasserstein 空间 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ 的黎曼流形几何框架，将扩散模型（通过 JKO 方案分析）与流匹配（通过 Benamou-Brenier 公式分析）置于统一的几何视角下进行对比。

Result: 证明了扩散模型的去噪步骤与 JKO 离散化方案的一致性，同时表明流匹配学习的是 Wasserstein 测地线。确定了二者分别为“梯度流（初值问题）”与“测地线（边值问题）”的区别。

Conclusion: 扩散模型（Diffusion Models）与最优传输流匹配（Flow Matching）在 Wasserstein 几何框架下实现了统一：前者本质上是自由能的梯度流（初值问题），后者则是在该流形上的测地线（边值问题）。两者通过不同的路径实现概率演化，但在物理与几何意义上实现了内在等价。

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || π) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>


### [199] [An Introduction to Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24160)
*Elias Bareinboim,Junzhe Zhang,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过结构因果模型将因果推断与强化学习结合，提出了“因果强化学习（CRL）”， unifying了多种学习范式并开启了处理反事实推理和复杂因果决策的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断与强化学习在各自领域独立发展，但本质上都处理反事实关系。现有文献中缺乏对两者深层连接的数学化整合，导致难以解决更复杂的现实世界学习任务。

Method: 通过结构因果模型（SCM）将强化学习环境去耦为一组具有不同因果不变性的自治机制，从而将因果推断的逻辑嵌入强化学习的决策过程中。

Result: 提出了“因果强化学习（CRL）”的概念，统一了不同的学习模式，并定义了包括泛化策略学习、介入决策、模仿学习及反事实学习在内的多种学习任务，展示了该交叉学科的广泛潜力。

Conclusion: 因果因果强化学习（CRL）作为因果推断与强化学习的结合，不仅统一了现有的学习范式（如在线、离线学习等），还为处理更复杂的任务（如泛化策略学习、介入策略优化、模仿学习和反事实学习）提供了理论框架和新的分析维度。

Abstract: Causal inference provides a set of principles and tools that allow one to combine data and knowledge about an environment to reason with questions of counterfactual nature, i.e., what would have happened had reality been different, even when no data of this unrealized reality is currently available. Reinforcement learning provides methods to learn a policy that optimizes a specific measure (e.g., reward, regret) when the agent is deployed in an environment and pursues an exploratory, trial-and-error approach. These two disciplines have evolved independently and with virtually no interaction between them. We note that they operate over different aspects of the same building block, counterfactual relations, which makes them umbilically connected. Based on these observations, novel learning opportunities arise when this connection is explicitly acknowledged and mathematized. To realize this potential, we note that any environment where the RL agent is deployed can be decomposed as a collection of autonomous mechanisms with different causal invariances, parsimoniously modeled as a structural causal model; any standard RL setting implicitly encodes such a model. This formalization allows us to put under a unifying treatment different modes of learning, including online, off-policy, and causal calculus learning, which appear unrelated in the literature. However, these modalities are not exhaustive: we introduce several natural and pervasive classes of learning settings that entail novel dimensions of analysis. Specifically, we introduce and discuss through causal lenses generalized policy learning, where to intervene, imitation learning, and counterfactual learning. These tasks lead to a broader view of counterfactual learning and suggest great potential for studying causal inference and reinforcement learning side by side, which we call causal reinforcement learning (CRL).

</details>


### [200] [Data Scale, Not Latency, Shapes Cross-Lingual Encoder Transfer in Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24169)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了流式语音识别中多语言初始化与英语初始化的权衡，发现多语言初始化的优势仅存在于低数据量情境，并随数据增加呈幂律衰减；该选择与流式延迟等级和模型量化决策相对独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将流式语音识别模型适配到新语言时，对于选择多语言预训练模型还是单语言预训练模型存在不确定性。不清楚多语言预训练带来的优势在何种数据规模下持续，优势是否受流式延迟影响，以及在量化部署后的表现。

Method: 使用0.6B参数的缓存感知（cache-aware）FastConformer转换器模型，在8种欧洲语言上进行了受控实验。研究变量包括：5个数据规模（100h到2500h）、3种流式延迟等级及离线解码、以及4个公开测试集。对比了多语言（ML）初始化与英语（EN）初始化两种方式。

Result: 1. 优势与数据量关系：多语言初始化的优势是“数据受限”的。在低数据量（100h）下WER领先约4.21pp，随数据增加快速衰减，在2500h达到平衡（仅0.20pp差距）。2. 延迟影响：多语言优势在不同流式延迟等级下保持相对稳定。3. 量化表现：4-bit权重压缩可缩减约3倍模型大小，且WER损失较小（平均约0.5pp）。

Conclusion: 在使用语音识别模型时，多语言初始化仅在低资源（低数据量）环境下具有明显优势。随着训练数据的增加，该优势会按幂律衰减并最终消失。此外，多语言初始化对流式延迟不敏感，且量化带来的影响与初始化方式的选择是相互独立的。建议在低数据量时使用多语言初始化，在大数据量时模型选择则相对无关紧要。

Abstract: Adapting a streaming speech recognition model to a new language requires choosing between two plausible warm starts: a multilingual (ML) encoder or an English-only (EN) encoder. The common intuition is that the multilingual encoder should help most at low data, but it is unclear how long that advantage persists, whether tight streaming latency amplifies it, and whether it survives deployment quantization. We answer these questions with a controlled sweep of a 0.6 B-parameter cache-aware FastConformer transducer across eight European languages, up to five target-language data scales (100 h to 2500 h), three streaming tiers plus offline decoding, and up to four public test sets. The main result is that multilingual initialization is a data-limited advantage, not a latency-limited one. On FLEURS at 160 ms, the mean EN-ML word error rate (WER) gap falls from +4.21 percentage points (pp) at 100 h to +0.20 pp at 2500 h; a power-law fit summarizes this decay, with each doubling of target-language data roughly halving the remaining advantage. Across the three streaming tiers, the across-language mean EN-ML gap is approximately stable at each scale from 100 to 1000 h, and is near zero by 2500 h. Finally, 4-bit weight-only encoder quantization at the matched 560 ms streaming tier reduces the encoder footprint by about 3x, with an average FLEURS WER increase of about 0.5 pp. The resulting guideline is simple: use multilingual initialization in low-data regimes, treat the choice as effectively irrelevant at large data, and make latency and quantization decisions independently.

</details>


### [201] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou,Yufeng Liu,Ye Liu,Yong Xu,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NaviGen 框架，通过引入双重标识符编码用户历史行为，并利用 SFT+RL 训练流水线，实现了从用户偏好到高质量生成指令的自动化构建，解决了个性化 AIGC 中的意图对齐缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AIGC 模型依赖高质量的详细指令，但普通用户往往难以准确表达视觉需求，导致生成内容与用户真实偏好不匹配。需要一种能将用户交互历史转化为可执行生成指令的技术。

Method: 1. 提出双重标识符（Dual Identifier）：将协同过滤编码与文本语义编码耦合，统一为单一标记流（token stream）；2. 两阶段优化流程：首先通过进化搜索生成的监督数据进行有监督微调（SFT），学习偏好推理与指令写作；随后通过分层且自一致的奖励机制进行强化学习（RL），实现生成结果与用户意图的深度对齐。

Result: 在产品、游戏和短视频领域的实验表明，NaviGen 有效提升了个性化图文生成质量，增强了下一项预测（next-item prediction）的准确性，并生成的指令更具具体性、相关性和视觉可执行性。

Conclusion: NaviGen 通过提出双重标识符（Dual Identifier）表示和两阶段 SFT+RL 优化范式，成功解决了用户个性化需求提取与生成指令对齐的难题，在多个内容生成领域实现了性能提升。

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/NaviGen.

</details>


### [202] [Exploring the relationship between human-centric AI and firm idiosyncratic risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24224)
*Zhen-Yuan Ralph Liu,Yu-Ting Wang,Jia-Jia Yan,Shivam Gupta,Mihalis Giannakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了“以人为本的 AI（HCAI）”如何通过降低伦理风险与促进协同来减少企业异质性风险（IR）。实证结果显示 HCAI 能有效降低 IR，且该效果受数字化水平、高管持股、运营效率及 CEO IT 背景等因素调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对工业 5.0 中“以人为本的 AI（HCAI）”如何影响企业异质性风险（IR）探讨不足。IR 能反映投资者对公司差异化 AI 战略的反应，该研究旨在探索 HCAI 如何通过降低 AI 伦理风险与促进人机协同来影响企业风险。

Method: 基于情境 AI 理论与社会技术系统理论，采用 2015-2023 年中国上市公司面板数据，利用统计建模分析 HCAI 对异质性风险（IR）的影响及社会技术因素的调节作用。

Result: 研究发现 HCAI 与较低的 IR 显著相关。其中，数字化水平和高管持股增强了这种风险降低效应；相反，运营效率和具有 IT 背景的 CEO 反而削弱了该效应。

Conclusion: HCAI 有效降低了企业的异质性风险（IR），但该效应受到数字化程度、高管持股、运营效率及 CEO IT 背景等社会技术因素的调节。企业应在 AI 治理中注重人机协同与伦理合规，以实现降低财务风险的稳健经营。

Abstract: Despite the extensive discussions of human-centric AI (HCAI) in Industry 5.0, its effects on firms' idiosyncratic risks (IR) remains underexplored. This is an imperative issue for firms navigate financial risks during the current technological revolution, as IR reflects investor reactions to corporate heterogeneous AI strategies and implementations by isolating firm-level stock volatility from systematic factors. Integrating situated AI theory with social-technical systems theory, we conceptualise HCAI as a situated AI strategy that reduces AI-related ethical risks and fosters AI-Human synergies in firms' business operations, ultimately reducing IR by aligning with stakeholders' diverse expectations. Moreover, socio-technical factors, namely digitalisation, operational efficiency, executive shareholding, and CEOs with IT background, may moderate the HCAI-IR relationship. Using a multi-source panel dataset of Chinese listed firms from 2015 to 2023, we find that HCAI is associated with lower firm IR. Furthermore, digitalisation and executive shareholding strengthen this risk-reducing effect, whereas operational efficiency and CEOs with IT background surprisingly attenuate it. Our findings offer theoretical contributions and practical insights for both ethical AI governance and firm financial risk management in the AI era.

</details>


### [203] [FlowR2A: Learning Reward-to-Action Distribution for Multimodal Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24231)
*Xirui Li,Zhe Liu,Xiaoqing Ye,Wenhua Han,Yifeng Pan,Junyu Han,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowR2A 通过流匹配框架将奖励作为生成条件，统一了自动驾驶规划中的稠密监督与动态提案生成，实现了更安全、更高效的驾驶决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划中“评分法”（限制在固定动作词表）与“锚点法”（稀疏监督）之间存在矛盾，旨在克服两者各自的局限性以提升规划质量。

Method: 提出了一种名为 FlowR2A 的生成模型框架：1. 将模拟得到的奖励作为生成条件而非判别目标；2. 使用流匹配解码器（flow-matching decoder）学习奖励条件下的动作分布；3. 引入细粒度的每时间步奖励条件化和奖励噪声增强机制；4. 支持测试阶段的通过奖励引导和锚点采样进行控制。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 和 v2 基准测试中达到行业领先水平（SOTA），生成的决策提案质量显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FlowR2A 通过引入流匹配解码器，成功将评分法（scoring-based）的稠密监督优势与锚点法（anchor-based）的动态生成能力相结合，在自动驾驶规划中实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>


### [204] [SP-Mind: An Autonomous Reasoning Agent for Spatial Proteomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24235)
*Yucheng Yuan,Yuanfeng Ji,Zhongxiao Li,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SP-Mind 是一款能够通过自然语言指令自动执行空间蛋白质组学全流程分析的 AI 智能体，配合新建立的 SP-Bench 基准，实现了该领域分析工作的标准化与自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间蛋白质组学分析流程分散，依赖专家手动操作，导致工具集成困难、研究可扩展性差且难以保证可重复性。

Method: 开发了 SP-Mind 自主 AI 智能体，能够将自然语言指令转化为端到端的空间蛋白质组学分析工作流；同时构建了包含 102 项任务的 SP-Bench 评估基准。

Result: SP-Mind 在 SP-Bench 测试中表现卓越，性能超越现有的开源生物医学智能体基准，能够高效完成从原始成像到表型发现的全流程分析。

Conclusion: SP-Mind 作为首个空间蛋白质组学自主 AI 智能体，通过统一的分析框架有效提升了研究的可扩展性和可重复性，在多个指标上达到业内最先进水平。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics enables single-cell-resolution characterization of protein expression within tissue architecture, playing a critical role in understanding tumor microenvironments and guiding precision medicine. However, current analysis workflows remain fragmented, requiring expert manual orchestration of heterogeneous tools and limiting research scalability and reproducibility. We present SP-Mind, the first autonomous AI agent designed to unify the spatial proteomics analysis pipeline, from raw multiplexed tissue imaging to downstream phenotype discovery. Equipped with expert-curated biological analysis skills and specialized computational tools, SP-Mind converts natural-language queries into end-to-end analytical workflows without task-specific fine-tuning. To rigorously evaluate its capabilities, we introduce SP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning diverse tissue types, comprising 102 tasks across 18 distinct categories. Through extensive evaluation on SP-Bench and established downstream tasks, SP-Mind achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing open-source biomedical agent baselines.

</details>


### [205] [Towards Federated Long-Tailed Graph Learning: An Energy-Guided Dual Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24237)
*Lianshuai Guo,Zhongzheng Yuan,Xunkai Li,Meixia Qu,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对联邦图学习中的长尾分布不平衡问题，提出 FedEPD 框架，通过拓扑净化与语义原型注入双重解耦策略，消除了结构噪声干扰，显著提升了模型对少数类别的识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习在处理长尾分布数据时，现有方法往往因无法恢复稀缺节点而导致过拟合结构噪声，模型偏向多数类且少数节点在异构邻域中被孤立。

Method: 提出了一种双重解耦框架 FedEPD：1. 利用基于狄利克雷能量的修剪技术过滤空间异质边（拓扑净化）；2. 从拓扑中心节点提取鲁棒全局原型，通过空间低通原型注入改善本地表示（语义校准）；3. 采用两阶段交替优化策略平衡多数与少数类别的决策边界。

Result: 在多个长尾数据集上达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平，准确率（Accuracy）提升最高达 4.97%，Macro-F1 指标提升最高达 5.48%。

Conclusion: FedEPD 通过解耦拓扑净化与语义校准，有效缓解了联邦图学习中长尾分布带来的偏差和结构噪音问题，在多项基准测试中达到了最优性能。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning facilitates collaborative graph modeling across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. However, real-world data categories frequently exhibit long-tailed distributions. Such statistical scarcity severely degrades performance in two ways: it biases the global model toward majority classes, and it structurally isolates minority nodes by submerging them in heterophilic, head-dominated neighborhoods. While existing methods attempt topology-agnostic statistical compensations, they often fail under data scarcity. Instead of recovering tail nodes, they overfit the structural noise from adjacent dominant classes, leading to representation degradation. To address these limitations, we propose FedEPD, a framework built on a dual decoupling paradigm that separates topological purification from semantic recalibration. Specifically, FedEPD utilizes distribution-aware Dirichlet energy pruning to filter spatial heterophilic edges. It then overcomes Non-IID distribution shifts by extracting robust global prototypes from topologically central nodes, which are incorporated into local representations via a spatial low-pass prototype injection. Furthermore, a two stage alternating optimization strategy strictly protects majority decision boundaries while improving minority accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedEPD achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-tailed benchmarks, yielding absolute improvements of up to 4.97% in Accuracy and 5.48% in Macro-F1.

</details>


### [206] [Probing the Misaligned Thinking Process of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24251)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Constantin Venhoff,Jonathan Michala,Xin Eric Wang,William Saunders*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过将大模型的不一致行为拆解为18个细粒度认知指标，并利用线性探测器对其进行内部激活监测，实现了一种高效、稳健的行为不一致性检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署于高风险场景时表现出的战略性欺骗、藏拙（sandbagging）和自我保护等不一致行为日益凸显，亟需可靠的检测方法以保障其安全负责地使用。

Method: 提出一种将不一致行为分解为18个细粒度认知指标（misalignment indicators）的方法；建立自动化、元计划引导的流水线生成多轮训练对话；利用线性探测器对模型内部激活状态进行检测；在包含自动化诱导、基准测试及良性对话的分布外（OOD）数据集上进行评估。

Result: 该探测方法在5种不一致行为上，于分布外测试集上达到了0.935的AUROC指标，且与强LLM评判者表现相当，同时在良性流量中保持了较低的误报率。

Conclusion: 通过将大模型的行为不一致性分解为细粒度的认知过程（18个指标），利用线性探测器（linear probes）可以高效、准确地监控和检测大模型的潜在不一致行为，在保证低误报率的前提下表现出优异的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a growing range of misaligned behaviors such as strategic deception, sandbagging, and self-preservation. As they are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, it is critical to reliably detect such behaviors to ensure safe and responsible use. In this work, we propose to monitor misalignment by decomposing it into fine-grained cognitive processes -- misalignment indicators -- and detecting their presence in a model's internal activations via linear probes. We develop a taxonomy of 18 indicators spanning different misaligned behaviors, paired with an automated, meta-plan-guided pipeline that generates multi-turn training conversations. To rigorously evaluate generalization, we construct an out-of-distribution suite combining automated behavioral elicitation, established misalignment benchmarks, and natural benign conversations. Across 5 misaligned behaviors, our probes match a strong LLM judge with 0.935 AUROC on out-of-distribution benchmarks while keeping a low false positive rate on benign traffic. We further perform in-depth analysis to understand the probes and the model's internal representations of misalignment indicators.

</details>


### [207] [Tractable Reasoning and Conjunctive Query Answering for Defeasible DL-Lite under Rational Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24279)
*Giovanni Casini,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的插件式架构，使得在 DL-Lite 系列描述逻辑中进行基于理性闭包（RC）的非单调逻辑推理和合取查询成为可能，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理性闭包（RC）是处理描述逻辑（DL）中可废止知识的常用非单调推理形式；作者旨在解决其如何在轻量级 DL-Lite 系列逻辑中高效实现及处理合取查询的问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用现有经典推理机作为底层的插件式架构，用于实现 DL-Lite 下的理性闭包（RC）推理。

Result: 成功将 RC 应用于 DL-Lite 的 Core 和 Horn 变体，并实现了对实例检查和合取查询的高效支持，证明了该方法在计算复杂度上的优势。

Conclusion: 通过引入一种插件式架构，证明了在 DL-Lite 逻辑簇（Core 和 Horn 变体）中，基于理性闭包（RC）的蕴含推理和合取查询（CQ）回答是高效的，且仅带来极小的额外计算开销。

Abstract: In Description Logics (DLs), reasoning under Rational Closure (RC) is a well-known and widely accepted non-monotonic formalism to handle defeasible knowledge. In this paper, we study the application of RC to the core and horn variants of the DL-Lite family of lightweight description logics. We analyze both entitlement (instance checking) and Conjunctive Query (CQ) answering under RC. Our main contribution is providing a plug-in architecture that builds upon existing standard classical reasoners, establishing that reasoning and CQ answering under RC for DL-Lite can be done efficiently with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [208] [LemonHarness Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24311)
*Kailong Ren,Fubo Sun,Jiachen Liu,Liu Yang,Zimo Yin,Jiaying Li,Congli Yin,Ming He,Yu Huo,Jiawei Liu,Zeping Chen,Yubin Huangfu,Ronghua Li,Yixuan Wu,Xing Su,Yanzhi Xu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Lei Zhang,Xiaohui Geng,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LemonHarness 是一个通过严格管控 workspace 状态变化、集成规则知识库及时间感知策略来提升长周期 LLM 智能体执行稳定性和成功率的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期 LLM 智能体在多轮迭代中频繁修改 workspace 状态，由于缺乏边界限制，状态变化难以追踪，且容易因缺乏执行反馈和时间管理导致任务失败。

Method: 提出了 LemonHarness 框架，构建明确的执行边界以约束系统状态变化（文件操作、环境配置等）；引入可重用的规则知识库以将执行规范转化为运行时知识；设计时间感知机制，向模型提供预算进度信息以优化任务策略。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，LemonHarness 结合 GPT-5.3-CodeX 达到 84.49% 的准确率，结合 GPT-5.5 则提升至 86.52%。

Conclusion: 统一的执行边界、可调用的规则知识库以及时间感知机制能够有效提升长周期智能体在复杂任务中的执行稳定性与准确性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are applied to longer tasks, they increasingly modify workspace state across multiple rounds of iteration. However, agents typically observe only tool outputs and log fragments, while the actual state changes occur in the file system. Without explicit workspace boundaries, state-changing operations such as file writes and temporary artifact generation may scatter changes across paths. Over time, these weakly constrained changes accumulate, making states such as modified files difficult to track. This paper presents LemonHarness, an integrated execution framework for long-horizon agents. LemonHarness establishes an explicit execution boundary by constraining state-changing operations within a clearly defined workspace and bringing model invocation, tool execution, and rule knowledge within a single controlled boundary. State-changing operations, including file writes, dependency installation, and temporary artifact creation, are executed through structured tool interfaces, with execution feedback recorded as observations available to subsequent model decisions. The system also introduces a reusable rule knowledge base, which turns recurring execution rules and acceptance criteria into runtime knowledge. LemonHarness further adds a time-aware execution mechanism that exposes elapsed and remaining budget to the model, so it can rebalance exploration, implementation, and validation effort as time pressure shifts and avoid timeouts from long waits or excessive verification. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, LemonHarness_GPT-5.3-CodeX reached 84.49% accuracy over 445 trials; pairing the same framework with the stronger GPT-5.5 backbone raised the average accuracy to 86.52% across five jobs. The results suggest that a unified runtime boundary, callable rule knowledge, and time-aware execution can improve the stability of long-horizon agent execution.

</details>


### [209] [Prob-BBDM: a Probabilistic Brownian Bridge Diffusion Model for MRI sequence image-to-image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24313)
*Martin Valls,Pascal Bourdon,Christine Fernandez-Maloigne,Guillaume Herpe,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于布朗桥扩散模型 (Prob-BBDM) 的高效 MRI 合成方法，通过变分编码器引导实现快速、高质量的跨模态影像生成，有效辅助临床分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中获取多模态 3D 医学影像资源密集且耗时，旨在通过低成本的 AI 合成技术生成高质量的 MRI 序列。

Method: 提出了一种基于布朗桥扩散模型 (BBDM) 的图像转换模型，并引入了变分编码器引导的扩散机制，利用概率图像分布来优化合成质量；该模型支持快速推理（仅需4步）。

Result: 在 BraTS 2021 数据集上表现优异（SSIM 高达 88.46%，PSNR 达 26.09 dB）；在外部数据集上展现了良好的泛化能力；下游肿瘤分割任务中 Dice 系数达到 88.71%，证明了合成数据保留了关键临床诊断信息。

Conclusion: Prob-BBDM 证明了在 MRI 合成任务中实现高质、高效及强泛化能力的潜力，为解决多模态医学影像获取资源密集的问题提供了一种有效方案，且合成图像具有极高的临床诊断可用性。

Abstract: AI-driven image-to-image synthesis is rapidly advancing, with growing applications in medical imaging. Multi-modal image analysis plays a crucial role in optimizing examination quality, yet acquiring multiple imaging modalities in clinical settings remains resource-intensive and time-consuming, especially for 3D imaging. To address this challenge, we propose a novel image-to-image translation model based on Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM), which synthesizes magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) sequences from 2D axial slices. Our approach integrates a variational encoder-guided diffusion mechanism, leveraging probabilistic image distributions to enhance synthesis quality. Evaluated on the BraTS 2021 dataset, our Probabilistic-BBDM (Prob-BBDM) achieves superior performance across multiple translation tasks, reaching up to 88.46% SSIM and 26.09 dB PSNR, with consistent improvements over baselines. Notably, our diffusion process requires only 4 steps, making it computationally efficient while maintaining high-quality synthesis. To further validate generalizability, we test Prob-BBDM on an external third-party dataset, demonstrating consistent performance across domains. Additionally, we assess the clinical utility of the synthesized slices by using them as input to a pre-trained segmentation model. Tumor segmentation yields a Dice score of 88.71% and an HD95 of 3.49 mm, confirming that the synthesized slices preserve critical diagnostic information. These results highlight the potential of Prob-BBDM for high-quality, efficient, and generalizable MRI synthesis, offering a promising step toward improved medical image translation.

</details>


### [210] [MVG-KAN: Multi-View Geo-Wind Guided KAN for PM$_{2.5}$ Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24347)
*Cheng Huang,Muyao Guan,Jairus Yougui Railey,Ning Xu,Honghui Xu,Changjiang Zhang,Zhen Zhang,Shiqing Zhang,Cong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MVG-KAN 模型，通过分解周期性与残差动力学，并构建结合地理与气象影响的“地理-风向图”及时序 KAN 网络，实现了更准确的 PM2.5 短期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 PM2.5 时空预测方法难以全面建模多源异构因素，尤其是忽略了风向对污染物传输的动态影响，以及传统图神经网络对空间关系建模的局限性。

Method: 提出 MVG-KAN 模型，包含三个核心部分：1. 周期-残差解耦架构，分离稳定规律与非周期变化；2. Geo-Wind Graph（地理-风向图），结合物理空间先验，以捕捉风向导向的污染物传输；3. TKAN（时序 Kolmogorov-Arnold Network）残差预测头，学习高精度的非线性自回归校正。

Result: 通过整合周期规律、物理驱动的路径先验和 KAN 的强非线性拟合能力，该方法能够更精准地捕捉 PM2.5 的短时波动及其在监测站点间的时空演化特征。

Conclusion: 提出了一种创新的 MVG-KAN 模型，通过多视角建模和物理驱动的图神经网络，有效地解决了 PM2.5 预测中复杂时空依赖和风向依赖污染物扩散的建模瓶颈。

Abstract: Accurate short-term PM$_{2.5}$ forecasting is important for public health protection, air-quality early warning, and urban environmental management. However, PM$_{2.5}$ variation is driven by multiple coupled factors, including stable periodic changes induced by human activities and meteorological regularity, station-specific short-term concentration evolution, and meteorology-driven pollutant dispersion among monitoring stations. Existing spatio-temporal forecasting methods may capture station relationships to some extent, but distance-only, correlation-based, or purely adaptive graphs are often insufficient to comprehensively represent these heterogeneous factors, especially wind-direction-dependent pollutant transport. To address this problem, we propose a Multi-View Geo-Wind Guided KAN model for PM$_{2.5}$ forecasting, named \textbf{MVG-KAN}, which models station-level PM$_{2.5}$ evolution from three complementary views: local periodic regularity, station-wise residual temporal dynamics, and meteorological-environment-guided spatial dispersion. Specifically, the periodic-residual forecasting backbone first separates stable daily and weekly patterns from non-periodic residual variations. A Geo-Wind Graph is constructed by combining geographic distance decay with wind-direction- and wind-speed-aware transport, providing a lightweight physically motivated directed spatial prior for residual propagation among stations. In addition, a temporal Kolmogorov-Arnold network (TKAN) residual head is then introduced to learn station-wise nonlinear autoregressive correction from de-periodized PM$_{2.5}$ residuals and historical multi-pollutant sequences, thereby enhancing the modeling of local residual inertia and pollutant co-variation.

</details>


### [211] [When Helpfulness Overrides Causal Caution: Context-Dependent Suppression and Recovery in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24370)
*Hiroshi Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在处理实际咨询任务时，受“乐于助人”模式驱动，容易在证据不足时草率下因果结论（缺乏“因果谨慎”），但通过针对性的自省提示或分离式架构可有效改善这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM的因果推理能力，但缺乏对其“因果谨慎（Causal Caution）”——即在证据不足时拒绝做出因果判断——这一认知维度的评估，特别是在LLM应用于现实建议场景时。

Method: 基于Pearl因果层级（PCH）构建评分指标；在学术与实际咨询两种语境下测试4款主流LLM（Claude 3.5 Sonnet/3 Opus、GPT-4o/5.5、Gemini 1.5 Pro/3.1），共480次实验；并通过自我修正提示（Self-correction prompt）进行干预测试。

Result: 在学术语境中，LLM因果谨慎维持率为91.7%-100%，但在实际咨询语境下降至6.7%-18.3%；而在请求具象化建议时，谨慎度降至0.5%。特定自我修正指令可将谨慎度恢复至71.4%-100%。

Conclusion: LLMs在实际咨询场景中表现出的“因果轻率”并非因果推理能力的缺失，而是由于“乐于助人”的响应模式导致的表达抑制。采用将提案生成与因果审核分离的多智能体架构是有效的治理设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into decision-support roles in business and policy contexts. While prior benchmark studies have primarily evaluated LLMs' causal reasoning capabilities, a more fundamental epistemic dimension has been overlooked: Causal Caution, defined as the propensity to refrain from causal judgment when empirical evidence is insufficient. This study examines the systematic suppression of Causal Caution that occurs when LLMs shift from academic to practical advisory contexts. Using an evaluation rubric inspired by Pearl's Causal Hierarchy (the PCH score), we conducted experiments on four high-performance LLMs -- Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT 5.5, and Gemini 3.1 Pro -- across 480 trials. Causal Caution maintenance rates were 91.7--100.0% in academic contexts but dropped to 6.7--18.3% in practical advisory contexts (Fisher's exact test, p < .001 across all models). Furthermore, when restricted to practical prompts requesting concrete recommendations or explanatory rationales, only 1 of 200 responses (0.5%) maintained Causal Caution. A brief self-correction prompt -- "Please reconsider this judgment from the perspective of causal relationships" -- restored the expression of Causal Caution to maintenance rates of 71.4--100.0% (McNemar's test, p < .001 across all models). These results suggest that helpfulness-oriented response patterns may suppress the expression of Causal Caution in practical advisory contexts, with important implications for organizational governance. The findings indicate that this suppression reflects context-dependent variation in expression rather than an underlying capability limitation, suggesting that multi-agent architectures that separate proposal generation from causal auditing may offer a promising governance design.

</details>


### [212] [PHANTOM: A Large-Scale Dataset of Multimodal Adversarial Attacks for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24388)
*Simone Gallivanone,Hossein Khodadadi,Mauro Dore,Mauro Medda,Nicola Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者开源了一个包含4.7万余条对抗样本的大规模VLM安全数据集，旨在降低研究门槛，提升视觉语言模型在对抗环境下的安全性评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高质量对抗攻击样本的计算成本高且过程复杂，限制了视觉语言模型安全性和鲁棒性的深入研究。

Method: 整合现有基准，利用前沿攻击策略生成涵盖10个大类、55个子类的47,524个对抗样本，包含7,826个指令意图。

Result: 构建了一个包含47,524个对抗样本的开放数据集，不仅整合了现有资源，还扩展了涵盖范围，为模型评估、防御机制开发及压力测试提供了实用资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过开源大规模对抗攻击样本，降低了视觉语言模型（VLM）安全研究的门槛，促进了模型鲁棒性评估的标准化、可重复性和全面性。

Abstract: We introduce a large-scale, open-source dataset of pre-generated adversarial attacks for vision-language models (VLMs). The dataset is designed to be diverse, representative, and practical, extending existing benchmarks by covering 10 high-level categories and 55 subcategories of harmful intents. Our primary goal is to make adversarial data accessible to the research community, given the computational cost and complexity of generating large numbers of attacks. The dataset comprises 47 524 adversarial samples, generated using state-of-the-art attack strategies from recent literature. Our work complements existing efforts by consolidating and extending prior benchmarks from multiple established sources, resulting in 7 826 intents, and introduce an additional category to broaden coverage. This provides realistic evaluation resources for studying model robustness and alignment. Our dataset intends to enable researchers and practitioners to systematically evaluate the robustness and safety of VLMs, fine-tune attack-generation models, and develop or stress-test defensive guardrails under diverse adversarial conditions. By releasing this resource, we aim to lower the barrier to adversarial research and foster more reproducible, comprehensive, and comparable evaluations of VLM safety.

</details>


### [213] [Age of LLM: A Strategic 1v1 Benchmark for Reasoning, Diplomacy and Reliability of Large Language Models under Fog of War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24391)
*Arnaud Ricci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Age of LLM is a new 1v1 strategy benchmark designed to test LLM reasoning and belief-tracking under fog of war and diplomacy, revealing that models predominantly utilize 'nuclear rush' tactics and that illegal actions serve as an effective metric for evaluating state-awareness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To bypass data contamination prevalent in public benchmarks and to study how LLMs reason, track beliefs, and engage in deception within complex, adversarial, and uncertainty-heavy environments.

Method: Developed 'Age of LLM', a turn-based 1v1 strategy benchmark on a 13x7 grid featuring fog of war, full diplomacy mechanics, and strict JSON output requirements to test LLMs' strategic decision-making and reliability. 15 reasoning models were tested over 54 matches.

Result: The nuclear rush strategy dominates (85% win rate). Military conquest is rare but faster. While diplomacy is common, it rarely leads to successful cooperation. Furthermore, approximately 58% of illegal actions stem from fog/state-tracking errors, making them a proxy for measuring model belief-tracking capabilities.

Conclusion: LLMs' reliability is weakly linked to winning performance. The study highlights that the benchmark serves primarily as an analytical lens for examining LLM reasoning, strategic deception, and belief-tracking under adversarial uncertainty, rather than a definitive ranking tool.

Abstract: We introduce Age of LLM, a turn-based 1v1 benchmark in which two LLMs face off on a 13x7 grid to destroy the enemy base. Three stressors are deliberate: fog of war, full diplomacy (messages, ceasefires, ultimatums; uranium kept secret), and a reliability dimension where every turn must follow a strict JSON schema and an illegal action is silently discarded. The engine is private and each match uses a fresh random map seed and opponent, mitigating the data contamination that affects public benchmarks. Models receive a (near) rule-only prompt with no build-order advice (two tactical seed phrases were present during data collection; see Section 2.7). We benchmark 15 reasoning models across 54 matches and 5,258 actions.
